	[ProgId("WpfPLRegression.RibbonPlotWizardMgr")]
	public class RibbonPlotWizardMgr : IRibbonPlotWizardMgr
	{
		private static string[] supportedTypes = new string[14]
		{
			"T-xy", "P-xy", "T-x", "P-x", "y-x", "T-xx", "P-xx", "Prop-x", "Prop-T", "(y-x) vs. x",
			"Triangular", "Est. vs. Exp.", "Residual", "Deviation Plot"
		};

		private IHNode _hnode;

		private IHWizardPlot _wizardPlot;

		private static List<PWRPlotType> _lspwrTypes = new List<PWRPlotType>();

		private PWRParser _pwrParser = new PWRParser();

		private Assembly _asm;

		private Dictionary<string, IRibbonPlotWizardType> _dicTagIdToPlotType;

		private IHWizardPlot _tempWizard = (CHWizardPlot)Activator.CreateInstance(Marshal.GetTypeFromCLSID(new Guid("9E14C11F-6706-4067-8112-35CE9468F3A5")));

		public RibbonPlotWizardMgr()
		{
			//IL_0001: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_000b: Expected O, but got Unknown
			Initialize();
		}

		long IRibbonPlotWizardMgr.SupportedPlotTypes(ref List<string> lsTagIds)
		{
			lsTagIds.Clear();
			for (int i = 0; i < _lspwrTypes.Count; i++)
			{
				PWRPlotType val = _lspwrTypes[i];
				IRibbonPlotWizardType iplotType = null;
				_tempWizard = (CHWizardPlot)Activator.CreateInstance(Marshal.GetTypeFromCLSID(new Guid("9E14C11F-6706-4067-8112-35CE9468F3A5")));
				_pwrParser.FillDefaultPlotVal(val, ref _hnode, ref _tempWizard);
				if (DoesPlotTypeHaveData(val.TagId, ref iplotType, ref _tempWizard))
				{
					lsTagIds.Add(val.TagId);
				}
			}
			return _lspwrTypes.Count;
		}

		long IRibbonPlotWizardMgr.GetPlotType(string sTagId, ref IRibbonPlotWizardType plwType)
		{
			if (_dicTagIdToPlotType.ContainsKey(sTagId))
			{
				plwType = _dicTagIdToPlotType[sTagId];
				if (plwType == null)
				{
					return 0L;
				}
				PWRPlotType val = null;
				_pwrParser.FindPlotType(sTagId, ref _lspwrTypes, ref val);
				_pwrParser.FillDefaultPlotVal(val, ref _hnode, ref _wizardPlot);
			}
			return 0L;
		}

		void IRibbonPlotWizardMgr.SetContext(object oNode, ref object oWizard)
		{
			_hnode = oNode as IHNode;
			_wizardPlot = oWizard as IHWizardPlot;
		}

		void IRibbonPlotWizardMgr.ShowInputDialog(string sPlotName)
		{
		}

		private void InitializeWizardPlot()
		{
		}

		private void Initialize()
		{
			_asm = Assembly.GetExecutingAssembly();
			StreamReader streamReader = new StreamReader(Assembly.GetExecutingAssembly().GetManifestResourceStream("WpfPLRegression.Text.Regressionpwr.txt"));
			_lspwrTypes.Clear();
			_pwrParser.ParsePwrFile(streamReader, ref _lspwrTypes);
			_dicTagIdToPlotType = new Dictionary<string, IRibbonPlotWizardType>();
			string[] array = supportedTypes;
			foreach (string text in array)
			{
				if (text == supportedTypes[0])
				{
					IRibbonPlotWizardType value = (IRibbonPlotWizardType)(object)new GroupCompUnit("T-xy");
					_dicTagIdToPlotType.Add(text, value);
				}
				else if (text == supportedTypes[1])
				{
					IRibbonPlotWizardType value2 = (IRibbonPlotWizardType)(object)new GroupCompUnit("P-xy");
					_dicTagIdToPlotType.Add(text, value2);
				}
				else if (text == supportedTypes[2])
				{
					IRibbonPlotWizardType value3 = (IRibbonPlotWizardType)(object)new GroupCompUnit("T-x");
					_dicTagIdToPlotType.Add(text, value3);
				}
				else if (text == supportedTypes[3])
				{
					IRibbonPlotWizardType value4 = (IRibbonPlotWizardType)(object)new GroupCompUnit("P-x");
					_dicTagIdToPlotType.Add(text, value4);
				}
				else if (text == supportedTypes[4])
				{
					IRibbonPlotWizardType value5 = (IRibbonPlotWizardType)(object)new GroupComp("y-x");
					_dicTagIdToPlotType.Add(text, value5);
				}
				else if (text == supportedTypes[5])
				{
					IRibbonPlotWizardType value6 = (IRibbonPlotWizardType)(object)new GroupCompUnit("T-xx");
					_dicTagIdToPlotType.Add(text, value6);
				}
				else if (text == supportedTypes[6])
				{
					IRibbonPlotWizardType value7 = (IRibbonPlotWizardType)(object)new GroupCompUnit("P-xx");
					_dicTagIdToPlotType.Add(text, value7);
				}
				else if (text == supportedTypes[7])
				{
					IRibbonPlotWizardType value8 = (IRibbonPlotWizardType)(object)new PropertyXS3();
					_dicTagIdToPlotType.Add(text, value8);
				}
				else if (text == supportedTypes[8])
				{
					IRibbonPlotWizardType value9 = (IRibbonPlotWizardType)(object)new PropertyS3();
					_dicTagIdToPlotType.Add(text, value9);
				}
				else if (text == supportedTypes[9])
				{
					IRibbonPlotWizardType value10 = (IRibbonPlotWizardType)(object)new GroupComp("(y-x) vs. x");
					_dicTagIdToPlotType.Add(text, value10);
				}
				else if (text == supportedTypes[10])
				{
					IRibbonPlotWizardType value11 = (IRibbonPlotWizardType)(object)new TriangleS3();
					_dicTagIdToPlotType.Add(text, value11);
				}
				else if (text == supportedTypes[11])
				{
					IRibbonPlotWizardType value12 = (IRibbonPlotWizardType)(object)new ResidualS3_new("Est vs. Exp");
					_dicTagIdToPlotType.Add(text, value12);
				}
				else if (text == supportedTypes[12])
				{
					IRibbonPlotWizardType value13 = (IRibbonPlotWizardType)(object)new ResidualS3("Residual");
					_dicTagIdToPlotType.Add(text, value13);
				}
				else if (text == supportedTypes[13])
				{
					IRibbonPlotWizardType value14 = (IRibbonPlotWizardType)(object)new DeviationPlot("Deviation Plot");
					_dicTagIdToPlotType.Add(text, value14);
				}
			}
		}

		private bool DoesPlotTypeHaveData(string sTagId, ref IRibbonPlotWizardType iplotType, ref IHWizardPlot iWizardPlot)
		{
			int num = 0;
			if (_dicTagIdToPlotType.ContainsKey(sTagId))
			{
				iplotType = _dicTagIdToPlotType[sTagId];
				if (iplotType == null)
				{
					return false;
				}
				object obj = iWizardPlot;
				iplotType.Context((object)_hnode, ref obj, ref num);
				if (num == 0)
				{
					return true;
				}
			}
			return false;
		}
	}

		public DeviationPlot(string type)
		{
			//IL_000c: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0016: Expected O, but got Unknown
			InitializeComponent();
			CommandBinding commandBinding = new CommandBinding(OKCommand, ExecuteOKCommand, CanExecuteOKCommand);
			CommandBinding commandBinding2 = new CommandBinding(CancelCommand, ExecuteCancelCommand);
			base.CommandBindings.Add(commandBinding);
			base.CommandBindings.Add(commandBinding2);
			_bHasInputDialog = true;
			_type = type;
			if (_imgIcon == null)
			{
				Assembly.GetExecutingAssembly();
				string name = "WpfPLRegression.Images.regressvs2.ico";
				Stream manifestResourceStream = Assembly.GetExecutingAssembly().GetManifestResourceStream(name);
				_imgIcon = new Bitmap(manifestResourceStream);
			}
			CurveList.Header = WpfPLRegressionResources.SelectProperty_Deviation;
			base.DataContext = this;
		}

		}

		void IRibbonPlotWizardType.Context(object HNode, ref object HWizardPlot, ref int ierror)
		{
			if (HWizardPlot == null || HNode == null)
			{
				ierror = 1;
				return;
			}
			SetVarNames();
			_context = HNode as IHNode;
			_wizardPlot = HWizardPlot as IHWizardPlot;
			if (!Build_DataGroupList())
			{
				ierror = 1;
			}
			else if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_currentConfigValue))
			{
				RestoreConfigValue(_currentConfigValue);
			}
		}

		private IEnumerable<string> RestoreCurves(string ConfigString, List<PropertyTypeItem> items)
		{
			string[] array = ConfigString.Split(';');
			string[] array2 = array[2].Substring(array[2].IndexOf('=') + 1).Split(',');
			string[] array3 = array2;
			foreach (string curve in array3)
			{
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(curve))
				{
					items.FirstOrDefault((PropertyTypeItem p) => p.TypeName.Equals(curve));
					yield return curve;
				}
			}
		}

		public void UpdateWizardData()
		{
			if (!Build_DataGroupList())
			{
				_wizardPlot.RemoveAllDependentVariable();
			}
			else if (VariableList.Count <= 0)
			{
				_wizardPlot.set_ErrorStatus(3, true);
			}
			else
			{
				if (!groupTypeDic.ContainsKey(SelectedVariable))
				{
					return;
				}
				List<PropertyTypeItem> list = groupTypeDic[SelectedVariable];
				_context.FindNode(VarNames[2]);
				_context.FindNode(VarNames[3]);
				IHNode iHNode = _context.FindNode(VarNames[4]);
				IHNode iHNode2 = _context.FindNode(VarNames[5]);
				IHNode iHNode3 = _context.FindNode(VarNames[9]);
				IHNode iHNode4 = _context.FindNode(VarNames[8]);
				List<string> propertys = new List<string>();
				foreach (AesCheckListBoxItem c in CurveList.Curves)
				{
					if (c.IsChecked && list.FirstOrDefault((PropertyTypeItem p) => p.TypeName.Equals(c.Text)) != null)
					{
						propertys.Add(c.Text);
					}
					c.IsChecked = false;
				}
				_ = SelectedxAxisType;
				if (propertys.Count < 1 && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(_currentConfigValue))
				{
					propertys = RestoreCurves(_currentConfigValue, list).ToList();
				}
				_help.CloseForm((UserControl)this, (bool?)true);
				_wizardPlot.RemoveAllDependentVariable();
				_wizardPlot.PlotVal.NPoints = 0;
				_wizardPlot.PlotVal.NVariables = propertys.Count;
				_wizardPlot.PlotVal.NXVariables = propertys.Count;
				_wizardPlot.PlotVal.LineStyle = 2;
				int num = 0;
				int num2 = -1;
				string text = string.Empty;
				bool flag = false;
				PropertyTypeItem propertyTypeItem = list.FirstOrDefault((PropertyTypeItem p) => p.TypeName.Equals("TEMP"));
				if (propertyTypeItem != null)
				{
					num2 = propertyTypeItem.StartIndex;
					text = (dynamic)iHNode4.Elements[num2, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].Elements[0, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].get_Value(Type.Missing);
					flag = true;
				}
				else if (proDataTempValues.Count > 0)
				{
					flag = true;
				}
				if (!flag)
				{
					return;
				}
				int i;
				for (i = 0; i < propertys.Count; i++)
				{
					int startIndex = list.FirstOrDefault((PropertyTypeItem p) => p.TypeName.Equals(propertys[i])).StartIndex;
					if (iHNode == null || iHNode.Elements.Count == 0)
					{
						return;
					}
					num = iHNode.Elements[startIndex, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].Elements[0, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].Elements.Count;
					_wizardPlot.PlotVal.NPoints += num;
					string label = SelectedVariable + " %ID% " + propertys[i];
					((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_Label(i, label);
					((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_CurveStyle(i, 15);
					int num3 = -1;
					for (int num4 = 0; num4 < num; num4++)
					{
						num3++;
						double num5 = 0.0;
						_ = (double?)(dynamic)iHNode2.Elements[startIndex, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].Elements[0, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].Elements[num4, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].get_Value(Type.Missing);
						double? num6 = (dynamic)iHNode3.Elements[startIndex, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].Elements[0, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].Elements[num4, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].get_Value(Type.Missing);
						if (num6.HasValue)
						{
							num5 = num6.Value;
							if (_isExistTemp)
							{
								if (SelectedxAxisType == WpfPLRegressionResources.DeviationPlot_ExpTemp)
								{
									((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_XData(i, num3, (short)1, (object)iHNode.Elements[num2, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].Elements[0, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].Elements[num4, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing]);
								}
								else
								{
									((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_XData(i, num3, (short)1, (object)iHNode2.Elements[num2, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].Elements[0, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].Elements[num4, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing]);
								}
							}
							else
							{
								if (num4 >= proDataTempValues.Count)
								{
									break;
								}
								((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_XData(i, num3, (short)0, (object)proDataTempValues[num4]);
							}
							((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_YData(i, num3, (short)0, (object)num5);
						}
						else
						{
							num3--;
							_wizardPlot.PlotVal.NPoints--;
						}
					}
				}
				_wizardPlot.PlotVal.Title = WpfPLRegressionResources.DeviationPlot_Title;
				string arg = ((!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text)) ? text : XAxisUnit);
				_wizardPlot.PlotVal.XAxisTitle = $"{SelectedxAxisType} {arg}";
				((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_YAxisTitle(Type.Missing, WpfPLRegressionResources.Deviation_Title);
				_currentConfigValue = SaveConfigValue(propertys);
			}
		}

		private void ExecuteOKCommand(object sender, ExecutedRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			UpdateWizardData();
			_help.CloseForm((UserControl)this, (bool?)true);
		}

		private void CanExecuteOKCommand(object sender, CanExecuteRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			int num = CurveList.GetSelectedCurves().Count();
			if (num == CurveList.Curves.Count)
			{
				CurveList.IsCheckAll = true;
			}
			else
			{
				string[] selectedCurves = CurveList.GetSelectedCurves();
				CurveList.IsCheckAll = false;
				CurveList.SetSelectedCurves(selectedCurves);
			}
			if (num == 0 || string.IsNullOrEmpty(SelectedVariable) || string.IsNullOrEmpty(SelectedxAxisType))
			{
				e.CanExecute = false;
			}
			else
			{
				e.CanExecute = true;
			}
		}

		private void ExecuteCancelCommand(object sender, ExecutedRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			_help.CloseForm((UserControl)this, (bool?)false);
		}

		private void SetVarNames()
		{
			VarNames = new string[10] { "Output/LINE7", "Output", "Output/LINE1", "Output/LINE3", "Output/ESTVALUE", "Output/EXP_VALUE", "Output/DIFF", "Output/LINE5", "Output/LINE6", "Output/PER_ERROR1" };
		}

		private bool Build_DataGroupList()
		{
			if (!Build_DataGroupVariableList())
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(SelectedVariable))
			{
				SelectedVariable = VariableList[0];
			}
			return true;
		}

		private bool Build_DataGroupVariableList()
		{
			groupTypeDic.Clear();
			ObservableCollection<string> observableCollection = new ObservableCollection<string>();
			IHNode iHNode = _context.FindNode(VarNames[2]);
			IHNode iHNode2 = _context.FindNode(VarNames[3]);
			IHNode iHNode3 = _context.FindNode(VarNames[4]);
			IHNode iHNode4 = _context.FindNode(VarNames[7]);
			if (iHNode == null || iHNode2 == null || iHNode3 == null || iHNode4 == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			int count = iHNode.Elements.Count;
			if (count == 0)
			{
				return false;
			}
			bool result = false;
			for (int i = 0; i < count; i++)
			{
				int num = 0;
				if (iHNode3.Elements[i, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].Elements.Count > 0)
				{
					num = iHNode3.Elements[i, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].Elements[0, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].Elements.Count;
				}
				if (num <= 0)
				{
					continue;
				}
				result = true;
				string text = iHNode.Elements[i, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].Elements.get_Label(0, 0, Type.Missing);
				string text2 = (dynamic)iHNode.Elements[i, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].Elements[0, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].get_Value(Type.Missing);
				string arg = (dynamic)iHNode2.Elements[i, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].Elements[0, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].get_Value(Type.Missing);
				string arg2 = (dynamic)iHNode4.Elements[i, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].Elements[0, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].get_Value(Type.Missing);
				string typeName = text2;
				if (text2 != "TEMP" && text2 != "PRESSURE")
				{
					typeName = $"{text2} {arg} {arg2}";
				}
				if (!groupTypeDic.ContainsKey(text))
				{
					groupTypeDic.Add(text, new List<PropertyTypeItem>
					{
						new PropertyTypeItem
						{
							TypeName = typeName,
							StartIndex = i
						}
					});
					if (!observableCollection.Contains(text))
					{
						observableCollection.Add(text);
					}
				}
				else
				{
					groupTypeDic[text].Add(new PropertyTypeItem
					{
						TypeName = typeName,
						StartIndex = i
					});
				}
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_selectedVariable) && !observableCollection.Contains(_selectedVariable))
			{
				VariableList = observableCollection;
				return false;
			}
			VariableList = observableCollection;
			return result;
		}

		private void Build_VariableList(string selectVal)
		{
			List<string> list = new List<string>();
			if (!groupTypeDic.ContainsKey(selectVal))
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (PropertyTypeItem item in groupTypeDic[selectVal])
			{
				if (item.TypeName != "TEMP")
				{
					list.Add(item.TypeName);
				}
			}
			CurveList.SetCurves(list.ToArray());
		}

		private void Build_XAxis(string selectVal)
		{
			if (!groupTypeDic.ContainsKey(selectVal))
			{
				return;
			}
			if (groupTypeDic[selectVal].Find((PropertyTypeItem p) => p.TypeName == "TEMP") != null)
			{
				_isExistTemp = true;
				XAxisTypeList = new ObservableCollection<string>
				{
					WpfPLRegressionResources.DeviationPlot_ExpTemp,
					WpfPLRegressionResources.DeviationPlot_RegTemp
				};
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(_selectedxAxisType) || !XAxisTypeList.Contains(SelectedxAxisType))
				{
					SelectedxAxisType = XAxisTypeList[0];
				}
				return;
			}
			_isExistTemp = false;
			if (groupTypeDic[selectVal].FirstOrDefault() == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			int startIndex = groupTypeDic[selectVal].First().StartIndex;
			int count = _context.FindNode(VarNames[4]).Elements[startIndex, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].Elements[0, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].Elements.Count;
			int num = -1;
			for (int num2 = 0; num2 < count; num2++)
			{
				string path = $"Output/Prop Data/PROFILE/{selectVal}/#{num2}";
				string text = _context.FindNode(path).get_Name(Type.Missing);
				if (text.Contains("TEMP"))
				{
					num = num2;
					if (text.Equals("Exp Val TEMP"))
					{
						XAxisTypeList = new ObservableCollection<string> { WpfPLRegressionResources.DeviationPlot_ExpTemp };
					}
					else if (text.Equals("Est Val TEMP"))
					{
						XAxisTypeList = new ObservableCollection<string> { WpfPLRegressionResources.DeviationPlot_RegTemp };
					}
					else
					{
						XAxisTypeList = new ObservableCollection<string> { text };
					}
					SelectedxAxisType = XAxisTypeList[0];
					break;
				}
			}
			if (num < 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			proDataTempValues = new List<double>();
			string path2 = $"Output/Prop Data/PROFILE/{selectVal}/#{num}/#{0}";
			XAxisUnit = _context.FindNode(path2).UnitString;
			for (int num3 = 0; num3 < count; num3++)
			{
				string path3 = $"Output/Prop Data/PROFILE/{selectVal}/#{num}/#{num3}";
				if (_context.FindNode(path3) != null)
				{
					proDataTempValues.Add((dynamic)_context.FindNode(path3).get_Value(Type.Missing));
				}
			}
		}

		private string SaveConfigValue(IEnumerable<string> propertys)
		{
			_ = string.Empty;
			StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
			stringBuilder.Append("DataGroup =");
			stringBuilder.Append(SelectedVariable);
			stringBuilder.Append(";");
			stringBuilder.Append("XAxis =");
			stringBuilder.Append(SelectedxAxisType);
			stringBuilder.Append(";");
			stringBuilder.Append("SelectedCurves =");
			foreach (string property in propertys)
			{
				stringBuilder.Append(property);
				stringBuilder.Append(",");
			}
			return stringBuilder.ToString();
		}

		private void RestoreConfigValue(string ConfigString)
		{
			string[] array = ConfigString.Split(';');
			int num = 0;
			string selectedVariable = array[num].Substring(array[num].IndexOf('=') + 1);
			num++;
			string selectedxAxisType = array[num].Substring(array[num].IndexOf('=') + 1);
			SelectedxAxisType = selectedxAxisType;
			SelectedVariable = selectedVariable;
		}

		private void FirePropertyChangedEvent(string propName)
		{
			if (this.PropertyChanged != null)
			{
				this.PropertyChanged(this, new PropertyChangedEventArgs(propName));
			}
		}

		public GroupComp(string type)
		{
			InitializeComponent();
			CommandBinding commandBinding = new CommandBinding(OKCommand, ExecuteOKCommand, CanExecuteOKCommand);
			CommandBinding commandBinding2 = new CommandBinding(CancelCommand, ExecuteCancelCommand);
			base.CommandBindings.Add(commandBinding);
			base.CommandBindings.Add(commandBinding2);
			_bHasInputDialog = true;
			_type = type;
			if (_imgIcon == null)
			{
				Assembly.GetExecutingAssembly();
				string name = "WpfPLRegression.Images.RegressyXX.ico";
				if (_type == "y-x")
				{
					name = "WpfPLRegression.Images.RegressYX.ico";
				}
				else if (_type == "(y-x) vs. x")
				{
					name = "WpfPLRegression.Images.RegressYXx.ico";
				}
				Stream manifestResourceStream = Assembly.GetExecutingAssembly().GetManifestResourceStream(name);
				_imgIcon = new Bitmap(manifestResourceStream);
			}
			CurveList.Header = WpfPLRegressionResources.GroupCompUnit_CurveHeader;
			base.DataContext = this;
		}

		void IRibbonPlotWizardType.Context(object HNode, ref object HWizardPlot, ref int ierror)
		{
			if (HWizardPlot == null || HNode == null)
			{
				ierror = 1;
				return;
			}
			SetVarNames();
			_context = HNode as IHNode;
			_wizardPlot = HWizardPlot as IHWizardPlot;
			if (_context == null)
			{
				ierror = 1;
				return;
			}
			bool flag = false;
			IHNode iHNode = _context.FindNode("Output/LINE9");
			if (iHNode == null || iHNode.Elements == null || iHNode.Elements.Count < 1)
			{
				AnaResultRadio.IsEnabled = false;
				RegResultRadio.IsChecked = true;
				flag = true;
			}
			IHNode iHNode2 = _context.FindNode("Input/ANALYSIS");
			if (iHNode2 != null && !flag)
			{
				if ((dynamic)iHNode2.get_Value(Type.Missing) == "YES")
				{
					AnaResultRadio.IsChecked = true;
				}
				else
				{
					RegResultRadio.IsChecked = true;
				}
			}
			if (!BuildAvailPlots(isAnalysis: false))
			{
				ierror = 1;
				return;
			}
			if (!Build_DatasetsList())
			{
				ierror = 1;
				return;
			}
			MoleType = _selectedBasis;
			if (_wizardPlot.basis == 5 || _wizardPlot.basis == 8)
			{
				((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_YAxisTitle(Type.Missing, MoleType + " " + _component);
				_wizardPlot.PlotVal.XAxisTitle = MoleType + " " + _component;
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_currentConfigValue))
			{
				RestoreConfigValue(_currentConfigValue);
			}
		}

		public void UpdateWizardData()
		{
			List<string> list = CurveList.GetSelectedCurves().ToList();
			if (list.Count == 0)
			{
				_wizardPlot.set_ErrorStatus(3, true);
				return;
			}
			int count = list.Count;
			List<IHNode> list2 = new List<IHNode>();
			List<IHNode> list3 = new List<IHNode>();
			for (int i = 0; i < count; i++)
			{
				IHNode iHNode = _context.FindNode(VarNames[2] + "/" + list[i]);
				if (iHNode == null || iHNode.Elements.Count == 0)
				{
					return;
				}
				list2.Add(iHNode);
				if (_bAnalysis)
				{
					iHNode = _context.FindNode(VarNames[3] + "/" + list[i]);
					if (iHNode == null || iHNode.Elements.Count == 0)
					{
						return;
					}
					list3.Add(iHNode);
				}
			}
			SelectGroup.Clear();
			for (int j = 0; j < count; j++)
			{
				SelectGroup.Add(list[j]);
			}
			BuildComponentList();
			Build_Node_Lookup();
			int num = Compons.IndexOf(_component);
			int num2 = 0;
			int num3 = 0;
			switch (_wizardPlot.basis)
			{
			case 5:
			{
				for (int l = 0; l < count; l++)
				{
					if (Yc[l, 0, num] != -1 && Xc[l, 0, num] != -1)
					{
						num2++;
						int num6 = list2[l].Elements.get_RowCount(1);
						if (num6 > num3)
						{
							num3 = num6;
						}
					}
					if (Yc[l, 1, num] == -1 || (Xc[l, 0, num] == -1 && Xc[l, 1, num] == -1))
					{
						continue;
					}
					num2++;
					int num7 = list2[l].Elements.get_RowCount(1);
					if (num7 > num3)
					{
						num3 = num7;
					}
					if (_bAnalysis)
					{
						num7 = list3[l].Elements.get_RowCount(1);
						if (num7 > num3)
						{
							num3 = num7;
						}
					}
				}
				break;
			}
			case 8:
			{
				for (int k = 0; k < count; k++)
				{
					if (YXc[k, 0, num] != -1 && Xc[k, 0, num] != -1)
					{
						num2++;
						int num4 = list2[k].Elements.get_RowCount(1);
						if (num4 > num3)
						{
							num3 = num4;
						}
					}
					if (YXc[k, 1, num] == -1 || (Xc[k, 0, num] == -1 && Xc[k, 1, num] == -1))
					{
						continue;
					}
					num2++;
					int num5 = list2[k].Elements.get_RowCount(1);
					if (num5 > num3)
					{
						num3 = num5;
					}
					if (_bAnalysis)
					{
						num5 = list3[k].Elements.get_RowCount(1);
						if (num5 > num3)
						{
							num3 = num5;
						}
					}
				}
				break;
			}
			}
			_wizardPlot.RemoveAllDependentVariable();
			_wizardPlot.PlotVal.NVariables = num2;
			_wizardPlot.PlotVal.NXVariables = num2;
			_wizardPlot.PlotVal.NPoints = num3;
			_wizardPlot.PlotVal.XAxisTitle = MoleType + " " + _component;
			((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_YAxisTitle(Type.Missing, MoleType + " " + _component);
			int num8 = 0;
			bool[] array = new bool[num2];
			for (int m = 0; m < num2; m++)
			{
				array[m] = false;
			}
			switch (_wizardPlot.basis)
			{
			case 5:
			{
				for (int num15 = 0; num15 < count; num15++)
				{
					int num16 = list2[num15].Elements.get_RowCount(1);
					int num17 = list2[num15].Elements.get_RowCount(1);
					if (_bAnalysis)
					{
						num17 = list3[num15].Elements.get_RowCount(1);
					}
					if (Yc[num15, 0, num] != -1 && Xc[num15, 0, num] != -1)
					{
						for (int num18 = 0; num18 < num16; num18++)
						{
							if (!array[num8])
							{
								((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_Label(num8, string.Format(WpfPLRegressionResources.GroupCompUnit_Exp_F_ID_Legend, list[num15]));
								((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_CurveStyle(num8, 15);
								array[num8] = true;
							}
							((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_XData(num8, num18, (short)1, (object)list2[num15].Elements[Xc[num15, 0, num], num18, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing]);
							((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_YData(num8, num18, (short)1, (object)list2[num15].Elements[Yc[num15, 0, num], num18, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing]);
						}
						num8++;
					}
					if (Yc[num15, 1, num] == -1 || (Xc[num15, 0, num] == -1 && Xc[num15, 1, num] == -1))
					{
						continue;
					}
					int num19 = ((Xc[num15, 1, num] != -1) ? 1 : 0);
					int num20 = num16;
					if (_bAnalysis && num19 == 1)
					{
						num20 = num17;
					}
					for (int num21 = 0; num21 < num20; num21++)
					{
						if (!array[num8])
						{
							((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_Label(num8, string.Format(WpfPLRegressionResources.GroupCompUnit_Est_F_ID_Legend, list[num15]));
							((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_CurveStyle(num8, 240);
							array[num8] = true;
						}
						if (_bAnalysis && num19 == 1)
						{
							((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_XData(num8, num21, (short)1, (object)list3[num15].Elements[Xc[num15, num19, num], num21, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing]);
							((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_YData(num8, num21, (short)1, (object)list3[num15].Elements[Yc[num15, 1, num], num21, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing]);
						}
						else
						{
							((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_XData(num8, num21, (short)1, (object)list2[num15].Elements[Xc[num15, num19, num], num21, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing]);
							((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_YData(num8, num21, (short)1, (object)list2[num15].Elements[Yc[num15, 1, num], num21, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing]);
						}
					}
					num8++;
				}
				break;
			}
			case 8:
			{
				for (int n = 0; n < count; n++)
				{
					int num9 = list2[n].Elements.get_RowCount(1);
					int num10 = list2[n].Elements.get_RowCount(1);
					if (_bAnalysis)
					{
						num10 = list3[n].Elements.get_RowCount(1);
					}
					if (YXc[n, 0, num] != -1 && Xc[n, 0, num] != -1)
					{
						for (int num11 = 0; num11 < num9; num11++)
						{
							if (!array[num8])
							{
								((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_Label(num8, string.Format(WpfPLRegressionResources.GroupCompUnit_Exp_F_ID_Legend, list[n]));
								((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_CurveStyle(num8, 15);
								array[num8] = true;
							}
							((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_XData(num8, num11, (short)0, (object)(double)(dynamic)list2[n].Elements[Xc[n, 0, num], num11, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].get_Value(Type.Missing));
							((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_YData(num8, num11, (short)0, (object)(double)(dynamic)list2[n].Elements[YXc[n, 0, num], num11, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].get_Value(Type.Missing));
						}
						num8++;
					}
					if (YXc[n, 1, num] == -1 || (Xc[n, 0, num] == -1 && Xc[n, 1, num] == -1))
					{
						continue;
					}
					int num12 = ((Xc[n, 1, num] != -1) ? 1 : 0);
					int num13 = num9;
					if (_bAnalysis && num12 == 1)
					{
						num13 = num10;
					}
					for (int num14 = 0; num14 < num13; num14++)
					{
						if (!array[num8])
						{
							string label = string.Format(WpfPLRegressionResources.GroupCompUnit_Est_F_ID_Legend, list[n]);
							((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_Label(num8, label);
							((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_CurveStyle(num8, 240);
							array[num8] = true;
						}
						if (_bAnalysis && num12 == 1)
						{
							((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_XData(num8, num14, (short)0, (object)(double)(dynamic)list3[n].Elements[Xc[n, num12, num], num14, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].get_Value(Type.Missing));
							((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_YData(num8, num14, (short)0, (object)(double)(dynamic)list3[n].Elements[YXc[n, 1, num], num14, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].get_Value(Type.Missing));
						}
						else
						{
							((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_XData(num8, num14, (short)0, (object)(double)(dynamic)list2[n].Elements[Xc[n, num12, num], num14, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].get_Value(Type.Missing));
							((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_YData(num8, num14, (short)0, (object)(double)(dynamic)list2[n].Elements[YXc[n, 1, num], num14, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].get_Value(Type.Missing));
						}
					}
					num8++;
				}
				break;
			}
			}
			if (_wizardPlot.basis == 5)
			{
				_wizardPlot.PlotVal.SquarePlot = true;
				_wizardPlot.PlotVal.ShowDiagonalLine = true;
			}
			_wizardPlot.PlotVal.ShowZeroLine = true;
			_currentConfigValue = SaveConfigValue();
		}

		private void ChangeBasisType()
		{
			Build_CandidateList();
			MoleType = _selectedBasis;
			if (_wizardPlot.basis == 5 || _wizardPlot.basis == 8)
			{
				((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_YAxisTitle(Type.Missing, MoleType + " " + _component);
				_wizardPlot.PlotVal.XAxisTitle = MoleType + " " + _component;
			}
		}

		private void SetVarNames()
		{
			VarNames = new string[5] { "Output/LINE7", "Output", "Output/Prop Data/PROFILE", "Output/Prop Data/PROFILE2", "Output/LINE9" };
		}

		private bool Build_DatasetsList()
		{
			if (Datasets == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			List<string> list = new List<string>();
			string[] datasets = Datasets;
			foreach (string item in datasets)
			{
				list.Add(item);
			}
			CurveList.SetCurves(list.ToArray());
			if (list.Count == 1)
			{
				CurveList.IsCheckAll = true;
			}
			else
			{
				CurveList.IsCheckAll = false;
			}
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(_selectedBasis))
			{
				SelectedBasis = _baseTypes[0];
			}
			return true;
		}

		private bool BuildAvailPlots(bool isAnalysis)
		{
			IHNode iHNode = _context.FindNode("Output/LINE7");
			if (_bAnalysis)
			{
				iHNode = _context.FindNode("Output/LINE9");
			}
			if (iHNode == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			int count = iHNode.Elements.Count;
			if (count == 0)
			{
				return false;
			}
			Datasets = new string[count];
			AvailPlots = new bool[count, 14, 4];
			for (int i = 0; i < count; i++)
			{
				for (int j = 0; j < 14; j++)
				{
					for (int k = 0; k < 4; k++)
					{
						AvailPlots[i, j, k] = false;
					}
				}
			}
			for (int l = 0; l < count; l++)
			{
				string text = iHNode.Elements.get_Label(0, l, Type.Missing);
				Datasets[l] = text;
				int basisIdxForDataset = _help.GetBasisIdxForDataset(_context, text);
				if (basisIdxForDataset < 0)
				{
					continue;
				}
				string text2 = (dynamic)iHNode.Elements[l, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].Elements["1", Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].get_Value(Type.Missing);
				text2 = text2.Trim();
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text2))
				{
					continue;
				}
				string[] array = text2.Split(' ');
				if (array == null)
				{
					continue;
				}
				for (int m = 0; m < array.Length; m++)
				{
					int result = 0;
					if (int.TryParse(array[m], out result) && result < AvailPlots.GetUpperBound(1))
					{
						AvailPlots[l, result, basisIdxForDataset] = true;
					}
				}
			}
			int plotType = 0;
			if (_wizardPlot.basis == 5)
			{
				plotType = 5;
			}
			if (_wizardPlot.basis == 8)
			{
				plotType = 10;
			}
			List<string> bsTypes = new List<string>();
			if (!BuildAvailBasis(plotType, ref bsTypes))
			{
				return false;
			}
			return true;
		}

		private bool BuildAvailBasis(int plotType, ref List<string> bsTypes)
		{
			AvailBasis = new bool[4];
			BaseTypes.Clear();
			bool flag = false;
			for (int i = AvailPlots.GetLowerBound(0); i <= AvailPlots.GetUpperBound(0); i++)
			{
				for (int j = 0; j <= 3; j++)
				{
					if (AvailPlots[i, plotType, j])
					{
						AvailBasis[j] = true;
						if (!flag)
						{
							flag = true;
						}
					}
				}
			}
			new List<string>();
			if (AvailBasis[0])
			{
				if (!_baseTypes.Contains(WpfPLRegressionResources.MassFraction_AxisTitle))
				{
					BaseTypes.Add(WpfPLRegressionResources.MassFraction_AxisTitle);
				}
				bsTypes.Add(WpfPLRegressionResources.MassFraction_AxisTitle);
			}
			if (AvailBasis[1])
			{
				if (!_baseTypes.Contains(WpfPLRegressionResources.MassPercent_AxisTitle))
				{
					BaseTypes.Add(WpfPLRegressionResources.MassPercent_AxisTitle);
				}
				bsTypes.Add(WpfPLRegressionResources.MassPercent_AxisTitle);
			}
			if (AvailBasis[2])
			{
				if (!_baseTypes.Contains(WpfPLRegressionResources.MoleFraction_AxisTitle))
				{
					BaseTypes.Add(WpfPLRegressionResources.MoleFraction_AxisTitle);
				}
				bsTypes.Add(WpfPLRegressionResources.MoleFraction_AxisTitle);
			}
			if (AvailBasis[3])
			{
				if (!_baseTypes.Contains(WpfPLRegressionResources.MolePercent_AxisTitle))
				{
					BaseTypes.Add(WpfPLRegressionResources.MolePercent_AxisTitle);
				}
				bsTypes.Add(WpfPLRegressionResources.MolePercent_AxisTitle);
			}
			return flag;
		}

		private bool Build_CandidateList()
		{
			IHNode iHNode = _context.FindNode("Output/LINE7");
			if (_bAnalysis)
			{
				iHNode = _context.FindNode("Output/LINE9");
			}
			if (iHNode.Elements.Count == 0)
			{
				return false;
			}
			AvailGroup.Clear();
			int num = 0;
			switch (_wizardPlot.basis)
			{
			case 5:
				num = 5;
				break;
			case 8:
				num = 10;
				break;
			}
			int selectedBasisIdx = _help.GetSelectedBasisIdx(_selectedBasis);
			for (int i = 0; i <= AvailPlots.GetUpperBound(0); i++)
			{
				if (AvailPlots[i, num, selectedBasisIdx])
				{
					AvailGroup.Add(Datasets[i]);
				}
			}
			CurveList.SetCurves(AvailGroup.ToArray());
			if (AvailGroup.Count == 1)
			{
				CurveList.IsCheckAll = true;
			}
			else
			{
				CurveList.IsCheckAll = false;
			}
			if (AvailGroup.Count <= 0)
			{
				return false;
			}
			return true;
		}

		private bool BuildComponentList()
		{
			return Build_ComponentList().Count > 0;
		}

		private List<string> Build_ComponentList()
		{
			Compons.Clear();
			_help.BuildComponentList(_context, _wizardPlot, Compons);
			if (Compons.Count > 0 && string.IsNullOrEmpty(_component))
			{
				_component = Compons[0];
			}
			return Compons;
		}

		private void Build_Node_Lookup()
		{
			int count = SelectGroup.Count;
			int count2 = Compons.Count;
			if (count < 1)
			{
				return;
			}
			Temp = new int[count, 2];
			Pres = new int[count, 2];
			Xc = new int[count, 2, count2];
			X2c = new int[count, 2, count2];
			Yc = new int[count, 2, count2];
			YXc = new int[count, 2, count2];
			for (int i = 0; i < count; i++)
			{
				for (int j = 0; j < 2; j++)
				{
					Temp[i, j] = -1;
					Pres[i, j] = -1;
					for (int k = 0; k < count2; k++)
					{
						Xc[i, j, k] = -1;
						X2c[i, j, k] = -1;
						Yc[i, j, k] = -1;
						YXc[i, j, k] = -1;
					}
				}
			}
			for (int l = 0; l < count; l++)
			{
				IHNode iHNode = _context.FindNode(VarNames[0] + "/" + SelectGroup[l]);
				int count3 = iHNode.Elements.Count;
				for (int m = 0; m < count3; m++)
				{
					string[] array = ((string)(dynamic)iHNode.Elements[m, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].get_Value(Type.Missing)).Split(' ');
					if (array == null)
					{
						continue;
					}
					List<string> list = new List<string>();
					for (int n = 0; n < array.Length; n++)
					{
						if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(array[n]))
						{
							list.Add(array[n]);
						}
					}
					array = list.ToArray();
					int num = 0;
					switch (array[0])
					{
					case "T":
						if (int.Parse(array[2]) == -1 || int.Parse(array[2]) == 1)
						{
							if (int.Parse(array[2]) == -1)
							{
								Temp[l, 0] = int.Parse(array[1]) - 1;
							}
							else
							{
								Temp[l, 1] = int.Parse(array[1]) - 1;
							}
						}
						else if (int.Parse(array[2]) == 2)
						{
							Temp[l, 0] = int.Parse(array[1]) - 1;
							Temp[l, 1] = int.Parse(array[1]);
						}
						break;
					case "P":
						if (int.Parse(array[2]) == -1 || int.Parse(array[2]) == 1)
						{
							if (int.Parse(array[2]) == -1)
							{
								Pres[l, 0] = int.Parse(array[1]) - 1;
							}
							else
							{
								Pres[l, 1] = int.Parse(array[1]) - 1;
							}
						}
						else if (int.Parse(array[2]) == 2)
						{
							Pres[l, 0] = int.Parse(array[1]) - 1;
							Pres[l, 1] = int.Parse(array[1]);
						}
						break;
					case "X":
						num = Compons.IndexOf(array[1]);
						if (num < 0)
						{
							break;
						}
						if (int.Parse(array[3]) == -1 || int.Parse(array[3]) == 1)
						{
							if (int.Parse(array[3]) == -1)
							{
								Xc[l, 0, num] = int.Parse(array[2]) - 1;
							}
							else
							{
								Xc[l, 1, num] = int.Parse(array[2]) - 1;
							}
						}
						else if (int.Parse(array[3]) == 2)
						{
							Xc[l, 0, num] = int.Parse(array[2]) - 1;
							Xc[l, 1, num] = int.Parse(array[2]);
						}
						break;
					case "X2":
						num = Compons.IndexOf(array[1]);
						if (num < 0)
						{
							break;
						}
						if (int.Parse(array[3]) == -1 || int.Parse(array[3]) == 1)
						{
							if (int.Parse(array[3]) == -1)
							{
								X2c[l, 0, num] = int.Parse(array[2]) - 1;
							}
							else
							{
								X2c[l, 1, num] = int.Parse(array[2]) - 1;
							}
						}
						else if (int.Parse(array[3]) == 2)
						{
							X2c[l, 0, num] = int.Parse(array[2]) - 1;
							X2c[l, 1, num] = int.Parse(array[2]);
						}
						break;
					case "Y":
						num = Compons.IndexOf(array[1]);
						if (num < 0)
						{
							break;
						}
						if (int.Parse(array[3]) == -1 || int.Parse(array[3]) == 1)
						{
							if (int.Parse(array[3]) == -1)
							{
								Yc[l, 0, num] = int.Parse(array[2]) - 1;
							}
							else
							{
								Yc[l, 1, num] = int.Parse(array[2]) - 1;
							}
						}
						else if (int.Parse(array[3]) == 2)
						{
							Yc[l, 0, num] = int.Parse(array[2]) - 1;
							Yc[l, 1, num] = int.Parse(array[2]);
						}
						break;
					case "Y-X":
						num = Compons.IndexOf(array[1]);
						if (num < 0)
						{
							break;
						}
						if (int.Parse(array[3]) == -1 || int.Parse(array[3]) == 1)
						{
							if (int.Parse(array[3]) == -1)
							{
								YXc[l, 0, num] = int.Parse(array[2]) - 1;
							}
							else
							{
								YXc[l, 1, num] = int.Parse(array[2]) - 1;
							}
						}
						else if (int.Parse(array[3]) == 2)
						{
							YXc[l, 0, num] = int.Parse(array[2]) - 1;
							YXc[l, 1, num] = int.Parse(array[2]);
						}
						break;
					}
				}
				if (!_bAnalysis)
				{
					continue;
				}
				iHNode = _context.FindNode(VarNames[4] + "/" + SelectGroup[l]);
				if (iHNode == null)
				{
					break;
				}
				count3 = iHNode.Elements.Count;
				for (int num2 = 0; num2 < count3; num2++)
				{
					string[] array2 = ((string)(dynamic)iHNode.Elements[num2, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].get_Value(Type.Missing)).Split(' ');
					if (array2 == null)
					{
						continue;
					}
					List<string> list2 = new List<string>();
					for (int num3 = 0; num3 < array2.Length; num3++)
					{
						if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(array2[num3]))
						{
							list2.Add(array2[num3]);
						}
					}
					array2 = list2.ToArray();
					int num4 = 0;
					switch (array2[0])
					{
					case "T":
						if (int.Parse(array2[2]) == 1)
						{
							Temp[l, 1] = int.Parse(array2[1]) - 1;
						}
						break;
					case "P":
						if (int.Parse(array2[2]) == 1)
						{
							Pres[l, 1] = int.Parse(array2[1]) - 1;
						}
						break;
					case "X":
						num4 = Compons.IndexOf(array2[1]);
						if (num4 >= 0 && int.Parse(array2[3]) == 1)
						{
							Xc[l, 1, num4] = int.Parse(array2[2]) - 1;
						}
						break;
					case "X2":
						num4 = Compons.IndexOf(array2[1]);
						if (num4 >= 0 && int.Parse(array2[3]) == 1)
						{
							X2c[l, 1, num4] = int.Parse(array2[2]) - 1;
						}
						break;
					case "Y":
						num4 = Compons.IndexOf(array2[1]);
						if (num4 >= 0 && int.Parse(array2[3]) == 1)
						{
							Yc[l, 1, num4] = int.Parse(array2[2]) - 1;
						}
						break;
					case "Y-X":
						num4 = Compons.IndexOf(array2[1]);
						if (num4 >= 0 && int.Parse(array2[3]) == 1)
						{
							YXc[l, 1, num4] = int.Parse(array2[2]) - 1;
						}
						break;
					}
				}
			}
		}

		private bool MT_Present(string mtype)
		{
			string path = "Output/" + mtype;
			return _context.FindNode(path).Elements.Count != 0;
		}

		private void DetermineMoleType()
		{
			if (MT_Present("MOLEFRAC"))
			{
				MoleType = WpfPLRegressionResources.MoleFraction_AxisTitle;
			}
			else if (MT_Present("MOLEPCT"))
			{
				MoleType = WpfPLRegressionResources.MolePercent_AxisTitle;
			}
			else if (MT_Present("MASSFRAC"))
			{
				MoleType = WpfPLRegressionResources.MassFraction_AxisTitle;
			}
			else if (MT_Present("MASSPCT"))
			{
				MoleType = WpfPLRegressionResources.MassPercent_AxisTitle;
			}
			else
			{
				MoleType = "";
			}
		}

		private string Join(string[] xW, string sFill, bool bTrail)
		{
			return ((PlotwizardHelper)_help).Join(xW, sFill, bTrail);
		}

		private string SaveConfigValue()
		{
			_ = string.Empty;
			_ = string.Empty;
			StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
			stringBuilder.Append("BasisSelection=");
			stringBuilder.Append(_selectedBasis);
			stringBuilder.Append(";");
			stringBuilder.Append("RadioButton=");
			stringBuilder.Append(_bAnalysis);
			stringBuilder.Append(";");
			stringBuilder.Append("AnalRadioButtonEnable=");
			bool isEnabled = AnaResultRadio.IsEnabled;
			stringBuilder.Append(isEnabled);
			stringBuilder.Append(";");
			stringBuilder.Append("DataGroup=");
			ObservableCollection<AesCheckListBoxItem> observableCollection = new ObservableCollection<AesCheckListBoxItem>(CurveList.Curves);
			int count = observableCollection.Count;
			for (int i = 0; i < count; i++)
			{
				if (i > 0)
				{
					stringBuilder.Append(",");
				}
				string text = observableCollection[i].Text;
				stringBuilder.Append(text);
			}
			stringBuilder.Append(";");
			stringBuilder.Append("CurveSelection=");
			List<string> list = CurveList.GetSelectedCurves().ToList();
			int count2 = list.Count;
			for (int j = 0; j < count2; j++)
			{
				if (j > 0)
				{
					stringBuilder.Append(",");
				}
				stringBuilder.Append(list[j]);
			}
			stringBuilder.Append(";");
			stringBuilder.Append("CheckAll=");
			stringBuilder.Append(CurveList.IsCheckAll);
			stringBuilder.Append(";");
			return stringBuilder.ToString();
		}

		private void RestoreConfigValue(string ConfigString)
		{
			string[] array = ConfigString.Split(';');
			int num = 0;
			string selectedBasis = array[num].Substring(array[num].IndexOf('=') + 1);
			SelectedBasis = selectedBasis;
			num++;
			selectedBasis = array[num].Substring(array[num].IndexOf('=') + 1);
			if (selectedBasis.Equals("true", StringComparison.CurrentCultureIgnoreCase))
			{
				AnaResultRadio.IsChecked = true;
			}
			else
			{
				RegResultRadio.IsChecked = true;
			}
			num++;
			selectedBasis = array[num].Substring(array[num].IndexOf('=') + 1);
			if (selectedBasis.Equals("true", StringComparison.CurrentCultureIgnoreCase))
			{
				AnaResultRadio.IsEnabled = true;
			}
			else
			{
				AnaResultRadio.IsEnabled = false;
				RegResultRadio.IsChecked = true;
			}
			num++;
			selectedBasis = array[num].Substring(array[num].IndexOf('=') + 1);
			string[] array2 = selectedBasis.Split(',');
			List<string> list = new List<string>();
			string[] array3 = array2;
			foreach (string item in array3)
			{
				list.Add(item);
			}
			CurveList.SetCurves(list.ToArray());
			num++;
			selectedBasis = array[num].Substring(array[num].IndexOf('=') + 1);
			string[] array4 = selectedBasis.Split(',');
			list = new List<string>();
			array3 = array4;
			foreach (string item2 in array3)
			{
				list.Add(item2);
			}
			CurveList.SetSelectedCurves(list.ToArray());
			num++;
			selectedBasis = array[num].Substring(array[num].IndexOf('=') + 1);
			CurveList.IsCheckAll = Convert.ToBoolean(selectedBasis);
		}

		private void ExecuteOKCommand(object sender, ExecutedRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (AnaResultRadio.IsChecked == true)
			{
				_bAnalysis = true;
			}
			else
			{
				_bAnalysis = false;
			}
			BuildAvailPlots(_bAnalysis);
			UpdateWizardData();
			((PlotwizardHelper)_help).CloseForm((UserControl)this, (bool?)true);
		}

		private void CanExecuteOKCommand(object sender, CanExecuteRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			int num = CurveList.GetSelectedCurves().Count();
			if (num == CurveList.Curves.Count)
			{
				CurveList.IsCheckAll = true;
			}
			else
			{
				string[] selectedCurves = CurveList.GetSelectedCurves();
				CurveList.IsCheckAll = false;
				CurveList.SetSelectedCurves(selectedCurves);
			}
			e.CanExecute = num != 0;
		}

		private void ExecuteCancelCommand(object sender, ExecutedRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			((PlotwizardHelper)_help).CloseForm((UserControl)this, (bool?)false);
		}

		void IPlotWizardTemplate.SetCaption(ref string caption)
		{
			caption = TooltipHeaderForRibbonItem;
		}

		void IPlotWizardTemplate.SetDefaultHeight(ref double defaultHeight)
		{
			defaultHeight = 250.0;
		}

		void IPlotWizardTemplate.SetDefaultWidth(ref double defaultWidth)
		{
			defaultWidth = 250.0;
		}

		void IPlotWizardTemplate.SetMinHeight(ref double minHeight)
		{
			minHeight = 300.0;
		}

		void IPlotWizardTemplate.SetMinWidth(ref double minWidth)
		{
			minWidth = 300.0;
		}

		{
			//IL_0047: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0051: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_0054: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)

		public GroupCompUnit(string type)
		{
			InitializeComponent();
			CommandBinding commandBinding = new CommandBinding(OKCommand, ExecuteOKCommand, CanExecuteOKCommand);
			CommandBinding commandBinding2 = new CommandBinding(CancelCommand, ExecuteCancelCommand);
			base.CommandBindings.Add(commandBinding);
			base.CommandBindings.Add(commandBinding2);
			_bHasInputDialog = true;
			_type = type;
			if (_imgIcon == null)
			{
				Assembly.GetExecutingAssembly();
				string iconResourcePath = GetIconResourcePath(type);
				Stream manifestResourceStream = Assembly.GetExecutingAssembly().GetManifestResourceStream(iconResourcePath);
				_imgIcon = new Bitmap(manifestResourceStream);
			}
			CurveList.Header = WpfPLRegressionResources.GroupCompUnit_CurveHeader;
			base.DataContext = this;
		}

		private string GetIconResourcePath(string type)
		{
			string result = "WpfPLRegression.Images.RegressTXY.ico";
			switch (type)
			{
			case "T-xy":
				result = "WpfPLRegression.Images.RegressTXY.ico";
				break;
			case "P-xy":
				result = "WpfPLRegression.Images.RegressPxy.ico";
				break;
			case "T-x":
				result = "WpfPLRegression.Images.RegressTX.ico";
				break;
			case "P-x":
				result = "WpfPLRegression.Images.RegressPx.ico";
				break;
			case "T-xx":
			case "P-xx":
				result = "WpfPLRegression.Images.RegressXX.ico";
				break;
			}
			return result;
		}

		List<string> IRibbonPlotWizardType.FieldDataOptionList(string sFieldName)
		{
			if (sFieldName.CompareTo(WpfPLRegressionResources.Component_Title) == 0)
			{
				BuildComponentList();
				return Compons;
			}
			if (sFieldName.CompareTo(WpfPLRegressionResources.Temperature_Title) == 0)
			{
				return TempUnits;
			}
			if (sFieldName.CompareTo(WpfPLRegressionResources.Pressure_Title) == 0)
			{
				return PressUnits;
			}
			return null;
		}

		void IRibbonPlotWizardType.GetFieldData(string sFieldName, ref string sCurrentVal)
		{
			if (sFieldName.CompareTo(WpfPLRegressionResources.Component_Title) == 0)
			{
				sCurrentVal = _component;
			}
			if (sFieldName.CompareTo(WpfPLRegressionResources.Temperature_Title) == 0)
			{
				if (_type == "T-xy" || _type == "T-x")
				{
					sCurrentVal = ((PlotwizardHelper)_help).GetUnitString(_context.Application.Tree, _wizardPlot);
					_temperature = sCurrentVal;
				}
				else
				{
					sCurrentVal = _temperature;
				}
			}
			if (sFieldName.CompareTo(WpfPLRegressionResources.Pressure_Title) == 0)
			{
				if (_type == "P-xy" || _type == "P-x")
				{
					sCurrentVal = ((PlotwizardHelper)_help).GetUnitString(_context.Application.Tree, _wizardPlot);
					_pressure = sCurrentVal;
				}
				else
				{
					sCurrentVal = _pressure;
				}
			}
		}

		void IRibbonPlotWizardType.SetFieldData(string sFieldName, string sValue)
		{
			if (sFieldName.CompareTo(WpfPLRegressionResources.Component_Title) == 0)
			{
				_component = sValue;
			}
			if (sFieldName.CompareTo(WpfPLRegressionResources.Temperature_Title) == 0)
			{
				if (_type == "T-xy" || _type == "T-x")
				{
					((PlotwizardHelper)_help).SaveUnitsComboText(_context.Application.Tree, _wizardPlot, sValue);
				}
				_temperature = sValue;
			}
			if (sFieldName.CompareTo(WpfPLRegressionResources.Pressure_Title) == 0)
			{
				if (_type == "P-xy" || _type == "P-x")
				{
					((PlotwizardHelper)_help).SaveUnitsComboText(_context.Application.Tree, _wizardPlot, sValue);
				}
				_pressure = sValue;
			}
		}

		void IRibbonPlotWizardType.ShowInputDialog()
		{
		}

		RibbonControlType IRibbonPlotWizardType.GetFieldCtrlType(string sField)
		{
			if (sField.CompareTo(WpfPLRegressionResources.Component_Title) != 0)
			{
				if (sField.CompareTo(WpfPLRegressionResources.Temperature_Title) != 0)
				{
					if (sField.CompareTo(WpfPLRegressionResources.Pressure_Title) != 0)
					{
						return (RibbonControlType)0;
					}
					return (RibbonControlType)1;
				}
				return (RibbonControlType)1;
			}
			return (RibbonControlType)1;
		}

		void IRibbonPlotWizardType.Context(object HNode, ref object HWizardPlot, ref int ierror)
		{
			if (HWizardPlot == null || HNode == null)
			{
				ierror = 1;
				return;
			}
			SetVarNames();
			_context = HNode as IHNode;
			_wizardPlot = HWizardPlot as IHWizardPlot;
			if (_context == null)
			{
				ierror = 1;
				return;
			}
			bool flag = false;
			IHNode iHNode = _context.FindNode("Output/LINE9");
			if (iHNode == null || iHNode.Elements == null || iHNode.Elements.Count < 1)
			{
				AnaResultRadio.IsEnabled = false;
				RegResultRadio.IsChecked = true;
				flag = true;
			}
			IHNode iHNode2 = _context.FindNode("Input/ANALYSIS");
			if (iHNode2 != null && !flag)
			{
				if ((dynamic)iHNode2.get_Value(Type.Missing) == "YES")
				{
					AnaResultRadio.IsChecked = true;
				}
				else
				{
					RegResultRadio.IsChecked = true;
				}
			}
			if (!BuildAvailPlots(isAnalysis: false))
			{
				ierror = 1;
				return;
			}
			if (!Build_DatasetsList())
			{
				ierror = 1;
				return;
			}
			MoleType = _selectedBasis;
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_currentConfigValue))
			{
				RestoreConfigValue(_currentConfigValue);
			}
		}

		private void InitUnit()
		{
			int num = 0;
			IHNode iHNode = _context.FindNode(VarNames[2] + "/" + AvailGroup[0]);
			if (_bAnalysis)
			{
				iHNode = _context.FindNode(VarNames[3] + "/" + AvailGroup[0]);
			}
			if (iHNode == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (TempUnits.Count == 0)
			{
				IHNodeCol iHNodeCol = null;
				string text = "";
				for (num = 0; num <= Temp.GetUpperBound(0); num++)
				{
					int num2 = Temp[num, 0];
					if (_bAnalysis)
					{
						num2 = Temp[num, 1];
					}
					if (num2 != -1)
					{
						iHNodeCol = ((dynamic)iHNode.Elements[num2, -7, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].get_AttributeValue((short)5, Type.Missing)).Elements;
						if (text == "")
						{
							text = (dynamic)iHNode.Elements[num2, -7, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].get_Value(Type.Missing);
						}
						int num3 = iHNodeCol.get_RowCount(0);
						for (num = 0; num < num3; num++)
						{
							TempUnits.Add(iHNodeCol.get_Label(0, num, Type.Missing));
						}
						_temperature = text;
						break;
					}
				}
			}
			if (PressUnits.Count != 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			IHNodeCol iHNodeCol2 = null;
			string text2 = "";
			for (num = 0; num <= Pres.GetUpperBound(0); num++)
			{
				int num4 = Pres[num, 0];
				if (_bAnalysis)
				{
					num4 = Pres[num, 1];
				}
				if (num4 != -1)
				{
					iHNodeCol2 = ((dynamic)iHNode.Elements[num4, -7, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].get_AttributeValue((short)5, Type.Missing)).Elements;
					if (text2 == "")
					{
						text2 = (dynamic)iHNode.Elements[num4, -7, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].get_Value(Type.Missing);
					}
					int num5 = iHNodeCol2.get_RowCount(0);
					for (num = 0; num < num5; num++)
					{
						PressUnits.Add(iHNodeCol2.get_Label(0, num, Type.Missing));
					}
					_pressure = text2;
					break;
				}
			}
		}

		public void UpdateWizardData()
		{
			List<string> list = CurveList.GetSelectedCurves().ToList();
			if (list.Count == 0)
			{
				_wizardPlot.set_ErrorStatus(3, true);
				return;
			}
			int count = list.Count;
			List<IHNode> list2 = new List<IHNode>();
			List<IHNode> list3 = new List<IHNode>();
			for (int i = 0; i < count; i++)
			{
				IHNode iHNode = _context.FindNode(VarNames[2] + "/" + list[i]);
				if (iHNode == null || iHNode.Elements.Count == 0)
				{
					return;
				}
				list2.Add(iHNode);
				if (_bAnalysis)
				{
					iHNode = _context.FindNode(VarNames[3] + "/" + list[i]);
					if (iHNode == null || iHNode.Elements.Count == 0)
					{
						return;
					}
					list3.Add(iHNode);
				}
			}
			BuildComponentList();
			Build_Node_Lookup();
			InitUnit();
			int num = Compons.IndexOf(_component);
			int num2 = 0;
			int num3 = 0;
			try
			{
				switch (_wizardPlot.basis)
				{
				case 1:
				case 3:
				{
					for (int num10 = 0; num10 < count; num10++)
					{
						if (_wizardPlot.basis == 1)
						{
							if (Temp[num10, 0] != -1 && Yc[num10, 0, num] != -1)
							{
								num2++;
								int num11 = list2[num10].Elements.get_RowCount(1);
								if (num11 > num3)
								{
									num3 = num11;
								}
							}
							if (Temp[num10, 1] != -1 && Yc[num10, 1, num] != -1)
							{
								num2++;
								int num12 = list2[num10].Elements.get_RowCount(1);
								if (num12 > num3)
								{
									num3 = num12;
								}
								if (_bAnalysis)
								{
									num12 = list3[num10].Elements.get_RowCount(1);
									if (num12 > num3)
									{
										num3 = num12;
									}
								}
							}
						}
						if (Temp[num10, 0] != -1 && Xc[num10, 0, num] != -1)
						{
							num2++;
							int num13 = list2[num10].Elements.get_RowCount(1);
							if (num13 > num3)
							{
								num3 = num13;
							}
						}
						if (_wizardPlot.basis == 1)
						{
							if (Temp[num10, 1] == -1 || Xc[num10, 1, num] == -1)
							{
								continue;
							}
							num2++;
							int num14 = list2[num10].Elements.get_RowCount(1);
							if (num14 > num3)
							{
								num3 = num14;
							}
							if (_bAnalysis)
							{
								num14 = list3[num10].Elements.get_RowCount(1);
								if (num14 > num3)
								{
									num3 = num14;
								}
							}
						}
						else
						{
							if (Xc[num10, 1, num] == -1 || (Temp[num10, 0] == -1 && Temp[num10, 1] == -1))
							{
								continue;
							}
							num2++;
							int num15 = list2[num10].Elements.get_RowCount(1);
							if (num15 > num3)
							{
								num3 = num15;
							}
							if (_bAnalysis)
							{
								num15 = list3[num10].Elements.get_RowCount(1);
								if (num15 > num3)
								{
									num3 = num15;
								}
							}
						}
					}
					break;
				}
				case 2:
				case 4:
				{
					for (int l = 0; l < count; l++)
					{
						if (_wizardPlot.basis == 2)
						{
							if (Pres[l, 0] != -1 && Yc[l, 0, num] != -1)
							{
								num2++;

									((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_XData(num16, num43, (short)1, (object)list3[num38].Elements[X2c[num38, num41, num], num43, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing]);
									((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_YData(num16, num43, (short)1, (object)list3[num38].Elements[Temp[num38, 1], num43, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing]);
									continue;
								}
								((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_XData(num16, num43, (short)1, (object)list2[num38].Elements[X2c[num38, num41, num], num43, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing]);
								if (Temp[num38, 0] != -1)
								{
									((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_YData(num16, num43, (short)1, (object)list2[num38].Elements[Temp[num38, 0], num43, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing]);
								}
								else if (Temp[num38, 1] != -1)
								{
									((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_YData(num16, num43, (short)1, (object)list2[num38].Elements[Temp[num38, 1], num43, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing]);
								}
							}
							num16++;
						}
						for (int num44 = 0; num44 < 2; num44++)
						{
							if (Xc[num38, num44, num] == -1 || (Temp[num38, 0] == -1 && Temp[num38, 1] == -1))
							{
								continue;
							}
							int num45 = num39;
							if (_bAnalysis && num44 == 1)
							{
								num45 = num40;
							}
							for (int num46 = 0; num46 < num45; num46++)
							{
								if (!array[num16])
								{
									string text10 = "";
									if (num44 == 0)
									{
										text10 = string.Format(WpfPLRegressionResources.GroupCompUnit_ExpX1_F_ID_Legend, list[num38]);
										((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_CurveStyle(num16, 15);
									}
									else
									{
										text10 = string.Format(WpfPLRegressionResources.GroupCompUnit_EstX1_F_ID_Legend, list[num38]);
										((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_CurveStyle(num16, 240);
									}
									((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_Label(num16, text10);
									array[num16] = true;
								}
								if (_bAnalysis && num44 == 1)
								{
									((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_XData(num16, num46, (short)1, (object)list3[num38].Elements[Xc[num38, num44, num], num46, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing]);
									((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_YData(num16, num46, (short)1, (object)list3[num38].Elements[Temp[num38, 1], num46, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing]);
									continue;
								}
								((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_XData(num16, num46, (short)1, (object)list2[num38].Elements[Xc[num38, num44, num], num46, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing]);
								if (Temp[num38, 0] != -1)
								{
									((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_YData(num16, num46, (short)1, (object)list2[num38].Elements[Temp[num38, 0], num46, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing]);
								}
								else if (Temp[num38, 1] != -1)
								{
									((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_YData(num16, num46, (short)1, (object)list2[num38].Elements[Temp[num38, 1], num46, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing]);
								}
							}
							num16++;
						}
					}
					break;
				}
				case 10:
				{
					_wizardPlot.PlotVal.XAxisTitle = MoleType + " " + _component;
					for (int num18 = 0; num18 < count; num18++)
					{
						int num19 = list2[num18].Elements.get_RowCount(1);
						int num20 = list2[num18].Elements.get_RowCount(1);
						if (_bAnalysis)
						{
							num20 = list3[num18].Elements.get_RowCount(1);
						}
						for (int num21 = 0; num21 < 2; num21++)
						{
							if (X2c[num18, num21, num] == -1 || (Pres[num18, 0] == -1 && Pres[num18, 1] == -1))
							{
								continue;
							}
							int num22 = num19;
							if (_bAnalysis && num21 == 1)
							{
								num22 = num20;
							}
							for (int num23 = 0; num23 < num22; num23++)
							{
								if (!array[num16])
								{
									string text = "";
									if (num21 == 0)
									{
										text = string.Format(WpfPLRegressionResources.GroupCompUnit_ExpX2_F_ID_Legend, list[num18]);
										((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_CurveStyle(num16, 15);
									}
									else
									{
										text = string.Format(WpfPLRegressionResources.GroupCompUnit_EstX2_F_ID_Legend, list[num18]);
										((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_CurveStyle(num16, 240);
									}
									((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_Label(num16, text);
									array[num16] = true;
								}
								if (_bAnalysis && num21 == 1)
								{
									((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_XData(num16, num23, (short)1, (object)list3[num18].Elements[X2c[num18, num21, num], num23, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing]);
									((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_YData(num16, num23, (short)1, (object)list3[num18].Elements[Pres[num18, 1], num23, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing]);
									continue;
								}
								((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_XData(num16, num23, (short)1, (object)list2[num18].Elements[X2c[num18, num21, num], num23, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing]);
								if (Pres[num18, 0] != -1)
								{
									((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_YData(num16, num23, (short)1, (object)list2[num18].Elements[Pres[num18, 0], num23, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing]);
								}
								else if (Pres[num18, 1] != -1)
								{
									((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_YData(num16, num23, (short)1, (object)list2[num18].Elements[Pres[num18, 1], num23, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing]);
								}
							}
							num16++;
						}
						for (int num24 = 0; num24 < 2; num24++)
						{
							if (Xc[num18, num24, num] == -1 || (Pres[num18, 0] == -1 && Pres[num18, 1] == -1))
							{
								continue;
							}
							int num25 = num19;
							if (_bAnalysis && num24 == 1)
							{
								num25 = num20;
							}
							for (int num26 = 0; num26 < num25; num26++)
							{
								if (!array[num16])
								{
									string text2 = "";
									if (num24 == 0)
									{
										text2 = string.Format(WpfPLRegressionResources.GroupCompUnit_ExpX1_F_ID_Legend, list[num18]);
										((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_CurveStyle(num16, 15);
									}
									else
									{
										text2 = string.Format(WpfPLRegressionResources.GroupCompUnit_EstX1_F_ID_Legend, list[num18]);
										((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_CurveStyle(num16, 240);
									}
									((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_Label(num16, text2);
									array[num16] = true;
								}
								if (_bAnalysis && num24 == 1)
								{
									((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_XData(num16, num26, (short)1, (object)list3[num18].Elements[Xc[num18, num24, num], num26, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing]);
									((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_YData(num16, num26, (short)1, (object)list3[num18].Elements[Pres[num18, 1], num26, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing]);
									continue;
								}
								((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_XData(num16, num26, (short)1, (object)list2[num18].Elements[Xc[num18, num24, num], num26, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing]);
								if (Pres[num18, 0] != -1)
								{
									((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_YData(num16, num26, (short)1, (object)list2[num18].Elements[Pres[num18, 0], num26, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing]);
								}
								else if (Pres[num18, 1] != -1)
								{
									((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_YData(num16, num26, (short)1, (object)list2[num18].Elements[Pres[num18, 1], num26, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing]);
								}
							}
							num16++;
						}
					}
					break;
				}
				case 5:
				case 6:
				case 7:
				case 8:
					break;
				}
			}
			catch
			{
			}
			switch (_wizardPlot.basis)
			{
			case 1:
			case 3:
			case 9:
				switch (TempUnits.IndexOf(_temperature))
				{
				case 0:
					_wizardPlot.set_unitcol(Type.Missing, 3);
					break;
				case 1:
					_wizardPlot.set_unitcol(Type.Missing, 2);
					break;
				case 2:
					_wizardPlot.set_unitcol(Type.Missing, 4);
					break;
				case 3:
					_wizardPlot.set_unitcol(Type.Missing, 5);
					break;
				}
				break;
			case 2:
			case 4:
			case 10:
				_wizardPlot.set_unitcol(Type.Missing, PressUnits.IndexOf(_pressure) + 1);
				break;
			}
			SetTitles();
			_currentConfigValue = SaveConfigValue();
		}

		private void ExecuteOKCommand(object sender, ExecutedRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (AnaResultRadio.IsChecked == true)
			{
				_bAnalysis = true;
			}
			else
			{
				_bAnalysis = false;
			}
			BuildAvailPlots(_bAnalysis);
			SetTitles();
			UpdateWizardData();
			((PlotwizardHelper)_help).CloseForm((UserControl)this, (bool?)true);
		}

		private void CanExecuteOKCommand(object sender, CanExecuteRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			int num = CurveList.GetSelectedCurves().Count();
			if (num == CurveList.Curves.Count)
			{
				CurveList.IsCheckAll = true;
			}
			else
			{
				string[] selectedCurves = CurveList.GetSelectedCurves();
				CurveList.IsCheckAll = false;
				CurveList.SetSelectedCurves(selectedCurves);
			}
			e.CanExecute = num != 0;
		}

		private void ExecuteCancelCommand(object sender, ExecutedRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			((PlotwizardHelper)_help).CloseForm((UserControl)this, (bool?)false);
		}

		private void DataGroupSelector_OKButtonClick(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			UpdateWizardData();
			((PlotwizardHelper)_help).CloseForm((UserControl)this, (bool?)true);
		}

		private void DataGroupSelector_CancelButtonClick(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			((PlotwizardHelper)_help).CloseForm((UserControl)this, (bool?)false);
		}

		}

		private void SetVarNames()
		{
			VarNames = new string[5] { "Output/LINE7", "Output", "Output/Prop Data/PROFILE", "Output/Prop Data/PROFILE2", "Output/LINE9" };
		}

		private bool Build_DatasetsList()
		{
			if (Datasets == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			List<string> list = new List<string>();
			string[] datasets = Datasets;
			foreach (string item in datasets)
			{
				list.Add(item);
			}
			CurveList.SetCurves(list.ToArray());
			if (list.Count == 1)
			{
				CurveList.IsCheckAll = true;
			}
			else
			{
				CurveList.IsCheckAll = false;
			}
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(_selectedBasis))
			{
				SelectedBasis = _baseTypes[0];
			}
			return true;
		}

				break;
			case 3:
				_wizardPlot.PlotVal.Title = string.Format(WpfPLRegressionResources.GroupCompUnit_T_x_Title, ((PlotwizardHelper)_help).Join(Compons.ToArray(), " ", false));
				_wizardPlot.PlotVal.XAxisTitle = MoleType + " " + _component;
				((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_YAxisTitle(Type.Missing, WpfPLRegressionResources.PropS3_XAxisTitle);
				break;
			case 4:
				_wizardPlot.PlotVal.Title = string.Format(WpfPLRegressionResources.GroupCompUnit_P_x_Title, ((PlotwizardHelper)_help).Join(Compons.ToArray(), " ", false));
				_wizardPlot.PlotVal.XAxisTitle = MoleType + " " + _component;
