		private bool BuildAvailPlots(bool isAnalysis)
		{
			IHNode iHNode = _context.FindNode("Output/LINE7");
			if (isAnalysis)
			{
				iHNode = _context.FindNode("Output/LINE9");
			}
			if (iHNode == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			int count = iHNode.Elements.Count;
			if (count == 0)
			{
				return false;
			}
			Datasets = new string[count];
			AvailPlots = new bool[count, 14, 4];
			for (int i = 0; i < count; i++)
			{
				for (int j = 0; j < 14; j++)
				{
					for (int k = 0; k < 4; k++)
					{
						AvailPlots[i, j, k] = false;
					}
				}
			}
			for (int l = 0; l < count; l++)
			{
				string text = iHNode.Elements.get_Label(0, l, Type.Missing);
				Datasets[l] = text;
				int basisIdxForDataset = _help.GetBasisIdxForDataset(_context, text);
				int result = 0;
				if (basisIdxForDataset < 0)
				{
					continue;
				}
				string text2 = (dynamic)iHNode.Elements[l, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].Elements["1", Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].get_Value(Type.Missing);
				text2 = text2.Trim();
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text2))
				{
					continue;
				}
				string[] array = text2.Split(' ');
				if (array == null)
				{
					continue;
				}
				for (int m = 0; m < array.Length; m++)
				{
					if (int.TryParse(array[m], out result) && result < AvailPlots.GetUpperBound(1))
					{
						AvailPlots[l, result, basisIdxForDataset] = true;
					}
				}
			}
			int plotType = GetPlotType(_wizardPlot.basis);
			List<string> bsTypes = new List<string>();
			if (!BuildAvailBasis(plotType, ref bsTypes))
			{
				return false;
			}
			return true;
		}

		private bool BuildAvailBasis(int plotType, ref List<string> bsTypes)
		{
			AvailBasis = new bool[4];
			BaseTypes.Clear();
			bool flag = false;
			for (int i = AvailPlots.GetLowerBound(0); i <= AvailPlots.GetUpperBound(0); i++)
			{
				for (int j = 0; j <= AvailPlots.GetUpperBound(2); j++)
				{
					if (AvailPlots[i, plotType, j])
					{
						AvailBasis[j] = true;
						if (!flag)
						{
							flag = true;
						}
					}
				}
			}
			if (AvailBasis[0])
			{
				if (!_baseTypes.Contains(WpfPLRegressionResources.MassFraction_AxisTitle))
				{
					BaseTypes.Add(WpfPLRegressionResources.MassFraction_AxisTitle);
				}
				bsTypes.Add(WpfPLRegressionResources.MassFraction_AxisTitle);
			}
			if (AvailBasis[1])
			{
				if (!_baseTypes.Contains(WpfPLRegressionResources.MassPercent_AxisTitle))
				{
					BaseTypes.Add(WpfPLRegressionResources.MassPercent_AxisTitle);
				}
				bsTypes.Add(WpfPLRegressionResources.MassPercent_AxisTitle);
			}
			if (AvailBasis[2])
			{
				if (!_baseTypes.Contains(WpfPLRegressionResources.MoleFraction_AxisTitle))
				{
					BaseTypes.Add(WpfPLRegressionResources.MoleFraction_AxisTitle);
				}
				bsTypes.Add(WpfPLRegressionResources.MoleFraction_AxisTitle);
			}
			if (AvailBasis[3])
			{
				if (!_baseTypes.Contains(WpfPLRegressionResources.MolePercent_AxisTitle))
				{
					BaseTypes.Add(WpfPLRegressionResources.MolePercent_AxisTitle);
				}
				bsTypes.Add(WpfPLRegressionResources.MolePercent_AxisTitle);
			}
			return flag;
		}

		private bool Build_CandidateList()
		{
			IHNode iHNode = _context.FindNode("Output/LINE7");
			if (_bAnalysis)
			{
				iHNode = _context.FindNode("Output/LINE9");
			}
			if (iHNode.Elements.Count == 0)
			{
				return false;
			}
			AvailGroup.Clear();
			int plotType = GetPlotType(_wizardPlot.basis);
			int selectedBasisIdx = _help.GetSelectedBasisIdx(_selectedBasis);
			for (int i = 0; i <= AvailPlots.GetUpperBound(0); i++)
			{
				if (AvailPlots[i, plotType, selectedBasisIdx])
				{
					AvailGroup.Add(Datasets[i]);
				}
			}
			CurveList.SetCurves(AvailGroup.ToArray());
			if (AvailGroup.Count == 1)
			{
				CurveList.IsCheckAll = true;
			}
			else
			{
				CurveList.IsCheckAll = false;
			}
			if (AvailGroup.Count <= 0)
			{
				return false;
			}
			return true;
		}

		private bool BuildComponentList()
		{
			if (Compons == null)
			{
				Compons = new List<string>();
			}
			else
			{
				Compons.Clear();
			}
			IHNode iHNode = _context.FindNode("Output/LINE1");
			IHNode iHNode2 = _context.FindNode("Output/LINE3");
			int count = iHNode.Elements.Count;
			List<string> list = CurveList.GetSelectedCurves().ToList();
			int count2 = list.Count;
			for (int i = 0; i < count2; i++)
			{
				for (int j = 0; j < count; j++)
				{
					string empty = string.Empty;
					if (iHNode.Elements[j, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].Elements.Count <= 0)
					{
						continue;
					}
					string text = iHNode.Elements[j, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].Elements.get_Label(0, 0, Type.Missing);
					if (!(list[i].ToString() == text))
					{
						continue;
					}
					empty = (dynamic)iHNode.Elements[j, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].Elements[0, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].get_Value(Type.Missing);
					if (empty != "TEMP" && empty != "PRESSURE")
					{
						if (iHNode2.Elements[j, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].Elements.Count > 0)
						{
							empty = (dynamic)iHNode2.Elements[j, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].Elements[0, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].get_Value(Type.Missing);
						}
						if (!Compons.Contains(empty) && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(empty))
						{
							Compons.Add(empty);
						}
					}
				}
			}
			Compons.Sort();
			if (Compons.Count > 0 && (string.IsNullOrEmpty(_component) || Compons.IndexOf(_component) < 0))
			{
				_component = Compons[0];
			}
			return Compons.Count > 0;
		}

		private int GetPlotType(int basis)
		{
			int result = basis;
			if (basis == 9 || basis == 10)
			{
				result = basis - 3;
			}
			return result;
		}

		private void Build_Node_Lookup()
		{
			List<string> list = CurveList.GetSelectedCurves().ToList();
			int count = list.Count;
			int count2 = Compons.Count;
			Temp = new int[count, 2];
			Pres = new int[count, 2];
			Xc = new int[count, 2, count2];
			X2c = new int[count, 2, count2];
			Yc = new int[count, 2, count2];
			YXc = new int[count, 2, count2];
			for (int i = 0; i < count; i++)
			{
				for (int j = 0; j < 2; j++)
				{
					Temp[i, j] = -1;
					Pres[i, j] = -1;
					for (int k = 0; k < count2; k++)
					{
						Xc[i, j, k] = -1;
						X2c[i, j, k] = -1;
						Yc[i, j, k] = -1;
						YXc[i, j, k] = -1;
					}
				}
			}
			for (int l = 0; l < count; l++)
			{
				IHNode iHNode = _context.FindNode(VarNames[0] + "/" + list[l]);
				int count3 = iHNode.Elements.Count;
				for (int m = 0; m < count3; m++)
				{
					string text = (dynamic)iHNode.Elements[m, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].get_Value(Type.Missing);
					string[] array = ((PlotwizardHelper)_help).ParseLine(text);
					int num = 0;
					if (array == null)
					{
						break;
					}
					switch (array[0])
					{
					case "T":
						if (int.Parse(array[2]) == -1 || int.Parse(array[2]) == 1)
						{
							if (int.Parse(array[2]) == -1)
							{
								Temp[l, 0] = int.Parse(array[1]) - 1;
							}
							else
							{
								Temp[l, 1] = int.Parse(array[1]) - 1;
							}
						}
						else if (int.Parse(array[2]) == 2)
						{
							Temp[l, 0] = int.Parse(array[1]) - 1;
							Temp[l, 1] = int.Parse(array[1]);
						}
						break;
					case "P":
						if (int.Parse(array[2]) == -1 || int.Parse(array[2]) == 1)
						{
							if (int.Parse(array[2]) == -1)
							{
								Pres[l, 0] = int.Parse(array[1]) - 1;
							}
							else
							{
								Pres[l, 1] = int.Parse(array[1]) - 1;
							}
						}
						else if (int.Parse(array[2]) == 2)
						{
							Pres[l, 0] = int.Parse(array[1]) - 1;
							Pres[l, 1] = int.Parse(array[1]);
						}
						break;
					case "X":
						num = Compons.IndexOf(array[1]);
						if (num < 0)
						{
							break;
						}
						if (int.Parse(array[3]) == -1 || int.Parse(array[3]) == 1)
						{
							if (int.Parse(array[3]) == -1)
							{
								Xc[l, 0, num] = int.Parse(array[2]) - 1;
							}
							else
							{
								Xc[l, 1, num] = int.Parse(array[2]) - 1;
							}
						}
						else if (int.Parse(array[3]) == 2)
						{
							Xc[l, 0, num] = int.Parse(array[2]) - 1;
							Xc[l, 1, num] = int.Parse(array[2]);
						}
						break;
					case "X2":
						num = Compons.IndexOf(array[1]);
						if (num < 0)
						{
							break;
						}
						if (int.Parse(array[3]) == -1 || int.Parse(array[3]) == 1)
						{
							if (int.Parse(array[3]) == -1)
							{
								X2c[l, 0, num] = int.Parse(array[2]) - 1;
							}
							else
							{
								X2c[l, 1, num] = int.Parse(array[2]) - 1;
							}
						}
						else if (int.Parse(array[3]) == 2)
						{
							X2c[l, 0, num] = int.Parse(array[2]) - 1;
							X2c[l, 1, num] = int.Parse(array[2]);
						}
						break;
					case "Y":
						num = Compons.IndexOf(array[1]);
						if (num < 0)
						{
							break;
						}
						if (int.Parse(array[3]) == -1 || int.Parse(array[3]) == 1)
						{
							if (int.Parse(array[3]) == -1)
							{
								Yc[l, 0, num] = int.Parse(array[2]) - 1;
							}
							else
							{
								Yc[l, 1, num] = int.Parse(array[2]) - 1;
							}
						}
						else if (int.Parse(array[3]) == 2)
						{
							Yc[l, 0, num] = int.Parse(array[2]) - 1;
							Yc[l, 1, num] = int.Parse(array[2]);
						}
						break;
					case "Y-X":
						num = Compons.IndexOf(array[1]);
						if (num < 0)
						{
							break;
						}
						if (int.Parse(array[3]) == -1 || int.Parse(array[3]) == 1)
						{
							if (int.Parse(array[3]) == -1)
							{
								YXc[l, 0, num] = int.Parse(array[2]) - 1;
							}
							else
							{
								YXc[l, 1, num] = int.Parse(array[2]) - 1;
							}
						}
						else if (int.Parse(array[3]) == 2)
						{
							YXc[l, 0, num] = int.Parse(array[2]) - 1;
							YXc[l, 1, num] = int.Parse(array[2]);
						}
						break;
					}
				}
				if (!_bAnalysis)
				{
					continue;
				}
				iHNode = _context.FindNode(VarNames[4] + "/" + list[l]);
				if (iHNode == null)
				{
					break;
				}
				count3 = iHNode.Elements.Count;
				for (int n = 0; n < count3; n++)
				{
					string text2 = (dynamic)iHNode.Elements[n, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].get_Value(Type.Missing);
					string[] array2 = ((PlotwizardHelper)_help).ParseLine(text2);
					int num2 = 0;
					if (array2 == null)
					{
						break;
					}
					switch (array2[0])
					{
					case "T":
						if (int.Parse(array2[2]) == 1)
						{
							Temp[l, 1] = int.Parse(array2[1]) - 1;
						}
						break;
					case "P":
						if (int.Parse(array2[2]) == 1)
						{
							Pres[l, 1] = int.Parse(array2[1]) - 1;
						}
						break;
					case "X":
						num2 = Compons.IndexOf(array2[1]);
						if (num2 >= 0 && int.Parse(array2[3]) == 1)
						{
							Xc[l, 1, num2] = int.Parse(array2[2]) - 1;
						}
						break;
					case "X2":
						num2 = Compons.IndexOf(array2[1]);
						if (num2 >= 0 && int.Parse(array2[3]) == 1)
						{
							X2c[l, 1, num2] = int.Parse(array2[2]) - 1;
						}
						break;
					case "Y":
						num2 = Compons.IndexOf(array2[1]);
						if (num2 >= 0 && int.Parse(array2[3]) == 1)
						{
							Yc[l, 1, num2] = int.Parse(array2[2]) - 1;
						}
						break;
					case "Y-X":
						num2 = Compons.IndexOf(array2[1]);
						if (num2 >= 0 && int.Parse(array2[3]) == 1)
						{
							YXc[l, 1, num2] = int.Parse(array2[2]) - 1;
						}
						break;
					}
				}
			}
		}

		private string AmtUnits(int utype, string GroupID, int nUnitRow, int nUnitCol, int npnts)
		{
			string path = VarNames[2] + "/" + GroupID;
			if (_bAnalysis)
			{
				path = VarNames[3] + "/" + GroupID;
			}
			IHNode iHNode = _context.FindNode(path);
			string text = string.Empty;
			List<double> list = new List<double>();
			double item = default(double);
			for (int i = 0; i < npnts; i++)
			{
				bool flag = iHNode.Elements[utype, i, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing] == null;
				if (!flag && !((flag | ((dynamic)iHNode.Elements[utype, i, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].get_ValueForUnit((short)nUnitRow, (short)nUnitCol) == null)) ? true : false))
				{
					dynamic val = ((dynamic)iHNode.Elements[utype, i, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].get_ValueForUnit((short)nUnitRow, (short)nUnitCol)).ToString("G5");
					if ((double.TryParse(val, out item)) && !list.Contains(item))
					{
						list.Add(item);
					}
				}
			}
			if (list.Count > 0)
			{
				list = list.OrderByDescending((double p) => p).ToList();
				double num = list[0];
				double num2 = list[list.Count - 1];
				text = $"{num2} --- {num}";
			}
			string text2 = ((nUnitRow == 22) ? _temperature : _pressure);
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text2))
			{
				text = (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text) ? text2 : (text + " " + text2));
			}
			return text;
		}

		private string PressureAmtUnits(string GroupID, int npnts)
		{
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_pressure))
			{
				if (_bAnalysis)
				{
					return AmtUnits(Pres[0, 1], GroupID, 20, PressUnits.IndexOf(_pressure) + 1, npnts);
				}
				return AmtUnits(Pres[0, 0], GroupID, 20, PressUnits.IndexOf(_pressure) + 1, npnts);
			}
			return null;
		}

		private string TemperatureAmtUnits(string GroupID, int npnts)
		{
			int num = TempUnits.IndexOf(_temperature);
			switch (num)
			{
			case 0:
				num = 3;
				break;
			case 1:
				num = 2;
				break;
			case 2:
				num = 4;
				break;
			case 3:
				num = 5;
				break;
			}
			if (_bAnalysis)
			{
				return AmtUnits(Temp[0, 1], GroupID, 22, num, npnts);
			}
			return AmtUnits(Temp[0, 0], GroupID, 22, num, npnts);
		}

		private string SaveConfigValue()
		{
			_ = string.Empty;
			StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
			stringBuilder.Append("BasisType=");
			stringBuilder.Append(_selectedBasis);
			stringBuilder.Append(";");
			stringBuilder.Append("RadioButton=");
			stringBuilder.Append(_bAnalysis);
			stringBuilder.Append(";");
			stringBuilder.Append("AnalRadioButtonEnable=");
			bool isEnabled = AnaResultRadio.IsEnabled;
			stringBuilder.Append(isEnabled);
			stringBuilder.Append(";");
			stringBuilder.Append("DataGroup=");
			ObservableCollection<AesCheckListBoxItem> observableCollection = new ObservableCollection<AesCheckListBoxItem>(CurveList.Curves);
			int count = observableCollection.Count;
			for (int i = 0; i < count; i++)
			{
				if (i > 0)
				{
					stringBuilder.Append(",");
				}
				string text = observableCollection[i].Text;
				stringBuilder.Append(text);
			}
			stringBuilder.Append(";");
			stringBuilder.Append("CurveSelection=");
			List<string> list = CurveList.GetSelectedCurves().ToList();
			int count2 = list.Count;
			for (int j = 0; j < count2; j++)
			{
				if (j > 0)
				{
					stringBuilder.Append(",");
				}
				stringBuilder.Append(list[j]);
			}
			stringBuilder.Append(";");
			stringBuilder.Append("CheckAll=");
			stringBuilder.Append(CurveList.IsCheckAll);
			stringBuilder.Append(";");
			return stringBuilder.ToString();
		}

		private void RestoreConfigValue(string ConfigString)
		{
			string[] array = ConfigString.Split(';');
			int num = 0;
			string selectedBasis = array[num].Substring(array[num].IndexOf('=') + 1);
			SelectedBasis = selectedBasis;
			num++;
			selectedBasis = array[num].Substring(array[num].IndexOf('=') + 1);
			if (selectedBasis.Equals("true", StringComparison.CurrentCultureIgnoreCase))
			{
				AnaResultRadio.IsChecked = true;
			}
			else
			{
				RegResultRadio.IsChecked = true;
			}
			num++;
			selectedBasis = array[num].Substring(array[num].IndexOf('=') + 1);
			if (selectedBasis.Equals("true", StringComparison.CurrentCultureIgnoreCase))
			{
				AnaResultRadio.IsEnabled = true;
			}
			else
			{
				AnaResultRadio.IsEnabled = false;
				RegResultRadio.IsChecked = true;
			}
			num++;
			selectedBasis = array[num].Substring(array[num].IndexOf('=') + 1);
			string[] array2 = selectedBasis.Split(',');
			List<string> list = new List<string>();
			string[] array3 = array2;
			foreach (string item in array3)
			{
				list.Add(item);
			}
			CurveList.SetCurves(list.ToArray());
			num++;
			selectedBasis = array[num].Substring(array[num].IndexOf('=') + 1);
			string[] array4 = selectedBasis.Split(',');
			list = new List<string>();
			array3 = array4;
			foreach (string item2 in array3)
			{
				list.Add(item2);
			}
			CurveList.SetSelectedCurves(list.ToArray());
			num++;
			selectedBasis = array[num].Substring(array[num].IndexOf('=') + 1);
			CurveList.IsCheckAll = Convert.ToBoolean(selectedBasis);
		}

		}

		public void InitializeComponent()
		{
			if (!_contentLoaded)
			{
				_contentLoaded = true;
				Uri resourceLocator = new Uri("/WpfPLRegression;component/groupcompunit.xaml", UriKind.Relative);
				Application.LoadComponent(this, resourceLocator);
			}
		}

		[DebuggerNonUserCode]
		[GeneratedCode("PresentationBuildTasks", "4.0.0.0")]
		[EditorBrowsable(EditorBrowsableState.Never)]
		void IComponentConnector.Connect(int connectionId, object target)
		{
			//IL_0047: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0051: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_0054: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_005e: Expected O, but got Unknown
			switch (connectionId)
			{
			case 1:
				AnaResultRadio = (RadioButton)target;
				break;
			case 2:
				RegResultRadio = (RadioButton)target;
				break;
			case 3:
				CurveList = (CurvesSelectorControl)target;
				break;
			case 4:
				OkButton = (AesCmdButton)target;
				break;
			case 5:
				CancelButton = (AesCmdButton)target;
				break;
			default:
				_contentLoaded = true;
				break;
			}
		}
	}

		public PropertyS3()
		{
			InitializeComponent();
			CommandBinding commandBinding = new CommandBinding(OKCommand, ExecuteOKCommand, CanExecuteOKCommand);
			CommandBinding commandBinding2 = new CommandBinding(CancelCommand, ExecuteCancelCommand);
			base.CommandBindings.Add(commandBinding);
			base.CommandBindings.Add(commandBinding2);
			_bHasInputDialog = true;
			if (_imgIcon == null)
			{
				Assembly.GetExecutingAssembly();
				string name = "WpfPLRegression.Images.RegressPropt.ico";
				Stream manifestResourceStream = Assembly.GetExecutingAssembly().GetManifestResourceStream(name);
				_imgIcon = new Bitmap(manifestResourceStream);
			}
			TemperatureKey = WpfPLAnalysisResources.TemperatureKey;
			PropertyKey = WpfPLAnalysisResources.PropertyKey;
			base.DataContext = this;
		}

		List<string> IRibbonPlotWizardType.FieldDataOptionList(string sFieldName)
		{
			if (sFieldName.CompareTo(TemperatureKey) == 0)
			{
				List<string> list = new List<string>();
				((PlotwizardHelper)_help).LoadXUnitsList(_context.Application.Tree, _wizardPlot, list);
				return list;
			}
			if (sFieldName.CompareTo(PropertyKey) == 0)
			{
				List<string> list2 = new List<string>();
				((PlotwizardHelper)_help).LoadUnitsList(_context.Application.Tree, _wizardPlot, list2);
				return list2;
			}
			return null;
		}

		void IRibbonPlotWizardType.GetFieldData(string sFieldName, ref string sCurrentVal)
		{
			if (sFieldName.CompareTo(TemperatureKey) == 0)
			{
				sCurrentVal = ((PlotwizardHelper)_help).GetXUnitString(_context.Application.Tree, _wizardPlot);
			}
			else if (sFieldName.CompareTo(PropertyKey) == 0)
			{
				sCurrentVal = ((PlotwizardHelper)_help).GetUnitString(_context.Application.Tree, _wizardPlot);
			}
		}

		void IRibbonPlotWizardType.SetFieldData(string sFieldName, string sValue)
		{
			if (sFieldName.CompareTo(TemperatureKey) == 0 && _currentTemperatureUnit.CompareTo(sValue) != 0)
			{
				_currentTemperatureUnit = sValue;
			}
			else if (sFieldName.CompareTo(PropertyKey) == 0)
			{
				_currentPropertyUnit = sValue;
			}
			UpdateWizardData();
		}

		}

		RibbonControlType IRibbonPlotWizardType.GetFieldCtrlType(string sField)
		{
			if (sField.CompareTo(TemperatureKey) != 0 && sField.CompareTo(PropertyKey) != 0)
			{
				return (RibbonControlType)0;
			}
			return (RibbonControlType)1;
		}

		void IRibbonPlotWizardType.Context(object HNode, ref object HWizardPlot, ref int ierror)
		{
			if (HWizardPlot == null || HNode == null)
			{
				ierror = 1;
				return;
			}
			SetVarNames();
			SetXPropLabels();
			_context = HNode as IHNode;
			_wizardPlot = HWizardPlot as IHWizardPlot;
			if (!Init_GroupList())
			{
				ierror = 1;
				return;
			}
			bool flag = false;
			IHNode iHNode = _context.FindNode("Output/LINE9");
			if (iHNode == null || iHNode.Elements == null || iHNode.Elements.Count < 1)
			{
				AnaResultRadio.IsEnabled = false;
				RegResultRadio.IsChecked = true;
				flag = true;
			}
			IHNode iHNode2 = _context.FindNode("Input/ANALYSIS");
			if (iHNode2 != null && !flag)
			{
				if ((dynamic)iHNode2.get_Value(Type.Missing) == "YES")
				{
					AnaResultRadio.IsChecked = true;
				}
				else
				{
					RegResultRadio.IsChecked = true;
				}
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_currentConfigValue))
			{
				SetCheckedValues(_currentConfigValue);
			}
		}

		public void UpdateWizardData()
		{
			Groups = CurveList.GetSelectedCurves().ToList();
			XGroups = new bool[Groups.Count, XPropLabels.Count];
			int num = new List<string>(XPropLabels).IndexOf(_selectedProperty);
			for (int i = 0; i < Groups.Count; i++)
			{
				for (int j = 0; j < XPropLabels.Count; j++)
				{
					if (j == num)
					{
						XGroups[i, j] = true;
					}
					else
					{
						XGroups[i, j] = false;
					}
				}
			}
			int count = Groups.Count;
			IHNode[] array = new IHNode[count];
			IHNode[] array2 = new IHNode[count];
			for (int k = 0; k < count; k++)
			{
				array[k] = _context.FindNode(VarNames[1] + "/Prop Data/PROFILE/" + Groups[k]);
				if (array[k] == null || array[k].Elements.Count == 0)
				{
					return;
				}
				if (_bAnalysis)
				{
					array2[k] = _context.FindNode(VarNames[1] + "/Prop Data/PROFILE2/" + Groups[k]);
					if (array2[k] == null || array2[k].Elements.Count == 0)
					{
						return;
					}
				}
			}
			Build_Node_Lookup();
			int num2 = 0;
			int num3 = 0;
			bool flag = false;
			for (int l = 0; l < count; l++)
			{
				for (int m = 0; m < Compons.Count; m++)
				{
					if (m > 0 && XProps[l, num, 0, m - 1] == XProps[l, num, 0, m])
					{
						flag = true;
						break;
					}
					if (XProps[l, num, 0, m] != -1 && Temp[l, 0] != -1)
					{
						num2++;
						int num4 = array[l].Elements.get_RowCount(1);
						if (num4 > num3)
						{
							num3 = num4;
						}
					}
					if (XProps[l, num, 1, m] == -1 || (Temp[l, 0] == -1 && Temp[l, 1] == -1))
					{
						continue;
					}
					num2++;
					int num5 = array[l].Elements.get_RowCount(1);
					if (num5 > num3)
					{
						num3 = num5;
					}
					if (_bAnalysis)
					{
						num5 = array2[l].Elements.get_RowCount(1);
						if (num5 > num3)
						{
							num3 = num5;
						}
					}
				}
			}
			_wizardPlot.RemoveAllDependentVariable();
			if (num2 == 0 || num3 == 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			_wizardPlot.PlotVal.NVariables = num2;
			_wizardPlot.PlotVal.NXVariables = num2;
			_wizardPlot.PlotVal.NPoints = num3;
			int num6 = 0;
			bool[] array3 = new bool[num2 + 1];
			for (int n = 0; n < num2; n++)
			{
				array3[n] = false;
			}
			for (int num7 = 0; num7 < count; num7++)
			{
				try
				{
					int num8 = array[num7].Elements.get_RowCount(1);
					int num9 = array[num7].Elements.get_RowCount(1);
					if (_bAnalysis)
					{
						num9 = array2[num7].Elements.get_RowCount(1);
					}
					for (int num10 = 0; num10 < Compons.Count; num10++)
					{
						if (XProps[num7, num, 0, num10] != -1 && Temp[num7, 0] != -1)
						{
							for (int num11 = 0; num11 < num8; num11++)
							{
								if (!array3[num6])
								{
									string text = string.Empty;
									string text2 = PressureAmtUnits(Groups[num7]);
									if (text2 != string.Empty)
									{
										text = " (" + text2 + ")";
									}
									string arg = (flag ? "" : Compons[num10]);
									((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_CurveStyle(num6, 15);
									((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_Label(num6, string.Format(WpfPLRegressionResources.GroupCompUnit_Exp_F_F_ID_Legend, arg, Groups[num7]) + text);
									array3[num6] = true;
								}
								((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_XData(num6, num11, (short)1, (object)array[num7].Elements[Temp[num7, 0], num11, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing]);
								((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_YData(num6, num11, (short)1, (object)array[num7].Elements[XProps[num7, num, 0, num10], num11, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing]);
							}
							num6++;
						}
						if (XProps[num7, num, 1, num10] != -1 && (Temp[num7, 0] != -1 || Temp[num7, 1] != -1))
						{
							int num12 = ((Temp[num7, 1] != -1) ? 1 : 0);
							new SortedDictionary<double, PlotPoint>();
							int num13 = 0;
							int num14 = num8;
							if (_bAnalysis && num12 == 1)
							{
								num14 = num9;
							}
							for (num13 = 0; num13 < num14; num13++)
							{
								if (!array3[num6])
								{
									string text3 = string.Empty;
									string text4 = PressureAmtUnits(Groups[num7]);
									if (text4 != string.Empty)
									{
										text3 = " (" + text4 + ")";
									}
									string text5 = (flag ? "" : Compons[num10]);
									((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_CurveStyle(num6, 240);
									((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_Label(num6, string.Format(WpfPLRegressionResources.GroupCompUnit_Est_F_ID_Legend, text5 + " " + Groups[num7]) + text3);
									array3[num6] = true;
								}
								if (_bAnalysis && num12 == 1)
								{
									((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_XData(num6, num13, (short)1, (object)array2[num7].Elements[Temp[num7, num12], num13, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing]);
									((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_YData(num6, num13, (short)1, (object)array2[num7].Elements[XProps[num7, num, 1, num10], num13, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing]);
								}
								else
								{
									((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_XData(num6, num13, (short)1, (object)array[num7].Elements[Temp[num7, num12], num13, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing]);
									((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_YData(num6, num13, (short)1, (object)array[num7].Elements[XProps[num7, num, 1, num10], num13, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing]);
								}
							}
							num6++;
						}
						if (flag)
						{
							break;
						}
					}
				}
				catch
				{
				}
			}
			((PlotwizardHelper)_help).SaveUnitsComboText(_context.Application.Tree, _wizardPlot, _currentPropertyUnit);
			((PlotwizardHelper)_help).SaveXUnitsComboText(_context.Application.Tree, _wizardPlot, _currentTemperatureUnit);
			SetAxisLabels();
			_currentConfigValue = SaveConfigValue();
		}

		private void ChangePropertyType()
		{
			Build_DataGroupList();
		}

		private void SetVarNames()
		{
			VarNames = new string[9] { "Output/LINE7", "Output", "Output/LINE1", "Output/LINE3", "Output/ESTVALUE", "Output/EXP_VALUE", "Output/DIFF", "Output/LINE5", "Output/LINE9" };
		}

		private void SetXPropLabels()
		{
			XPropLabels = new List<string>(new string[5] { "KVL", "ALPHA", "GAMMA", "TBUB", "PBUB" });
		}

		private bool Init_GroupList()
		{
			Groups.Clear();
			IHNode iHNode = _context.FindNode(VarNames[0]);
			if (_bAnalysis)
			{
				iHNode = _context.FindNode(VarNames[8]);
			}
			if (iHNode == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			int count = iHNode.Elements.Count;
			if (count == 0)
			{
				return false;
			}
			for (int i = 0; i < count; i++)
			{
				string item = iHNode.Elements.get_Label(0, i, Type.Missing);
				Groups.Add(item);
			}
			if (!Validate_CandidateList())
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (!Build_PropertyList())
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (!Build_DataGroupList())
			{
				return false;
			}
			return true;
		}

		private bool Validate_CandidateList()
		{
			if (!Build_ComponentList())
			{
				return false;
			}
			DetermineMoleType();
			AppendExtraProps();
			SetXGroups(isInit: true);
			Build_Node_Lookup();
			SetPropGroups();
			return true;
		}

		private void SetAxisLabels()
		{
			string xUnitString = ((PlotwizardHelper)_help).GetXUnitString(_context.Application.Tree, _wizardPlot);
			_wizardPlot.PlotVal.XAxisTitle = WpfPLAnalysisResources.TemperatureKey + " " + xUnitString;
			string unitString = ((PlotwizardHelper)_help).GetUnitString(_context.Application.Tree, _wizardPlot);
			((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_YAxisTitle(Type.Missing, _selectedProperty + " " + unitString);
		}

		private bool SetPropGroups()
		{
			PropGroups.Clear();
			bool result = false;
			string text = "8";
			switch (_wizardPlot.basis)
			{
			case 6:
				text = "8";
				break;
			case 7:
				text = "9";
				break;
			}
			for (int i = 0; i < Groups.Count; i++)
			{
				string path = VarNames[0] + "/" + Groups[i];
				if (_bAnalysis)
				{
					path = VarNames[8] + "/" + Groups[i];
				}
				IHNode iHNode = _context.FindNode(path);
				string text2 = (dynamic)iHNode.Elements["1", Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].get_Value(Type.Missing);
				text2 = text2.Trim();
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text2))
				{
					continue;
				}
				string[] array = ((PlotwizardHelper)_help).ParseLine(text2);
				for (int j = 0; j < array.Length; j++)
				{
					if (array[j] == text)
					{
						AddPropGroups(i);
						result = true;
						break;
					}
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		private void AddPropGroups(int index)
		{
			if (XProps == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			for (int i = 0; i < XPropLabels.Count; i++)
			{
				for (int j = 0; j < Compons.Count; j++)
				{
					bool flag = false;
					for (int k = 0; k < 2; k++)
					{
						if (XProps[index, i, k, j] != -1)
						{
							flag = true;
							AddPropGroupsItem(new string[3]
							{
								XPropLabels[i],
								Groups[index],
								Compons[j]
							});
						}
						if (flag)
						{
							break;
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}

		private void AddPropGroupsItem(string[] paraArray)
		{
			string item = ((PlotwizardHelper)_help).Join(paraArray, " ", false);
			if (!PropGroups.Contains(item))
			{
				PropGroups.Add(item);
			}
		}

		private void SetXGroups(bool isInit)
		{
			XGroups = new bool[Groups.Count, XPropLabels.Count];
			List<string> list = new List<string>(XPropLabels);
			int num = ((!isInit) ? list.IndexOf(_selectedProperty) : 0);
			for (int i = 0; i < Groups.Count; i++)
			{
				for (int j = 0; j < XPropLabels.Count; j++)
				{
					if (j == num)
					{
						XGroups[i, j] = true;
					}
					else
					{
						XGroups[i, j] = false;
					}
				}
			}
			if (!isInit)
			{
				return;
			}
			for (int k = 0; k < Groups.Count; k++)
			{
				for (int l = 0; l < XPropLabels.Count; l++)
				{
					XGroups[k, l] = true;
				}
			}
		}

		private bool Build_ComponentList()
		{
			Compons.Clear();
			IHNode iHNode = _context.FindNode("Output/LINE1");
			IHNode iHNode2 = _context.FindNode("Output/LINE3");
			int count = iHNode.Elements.Count;
			for (int i = 0; i < count; i++)
			{
				string text = string.Empty;
				if (iHNode.Elements[i, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].Elements.Count > 0)
				{
					text = (dynamic)iHNode.Elements[i, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].Elements[0, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].get_Value(Type.Missing);
				}
				if (text != "TEMP" && text != "PRESSURE")
				{
					if (iHNode2.Elements[i, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].Elements.Count > 0)
					{
						text = (dynamic)iHNode2.Elements[i, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].Elements[0, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].get_Value(Type.Missing);
					}
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text) && !Compons.Contains(text))
					{
						Compons.Add(text);
					}
				}
			}
			Compons.Sort();
			return Compons.Count > 0;
		}

		private bool Build_PropertyList()
		{
			Properties = UniqueProperties();
			bool flag = Properties.Count > 0;
			if (flag)
			{
				PropertyTypes.Clear();
				foreach (string property in Properties)
				{
					PropertyTypes.Add(property);
				}
				SelectedProperty = Properties[0];
			}
			return flag;
		}

		private List<string> UniqueProperties()
		{
			List<string> list = new List<string>();
			int count = PropGroups.Count;
			for (int i = 0; i < count; i++)
			{
				string[] array = ((PlotwizardHelper)_help).ParseLine(PropGroups[i]);
				if (!list.Contains(array[0]))
				{
					list.Add(array[0]);
				}
			}
			return list;
		}

		private bool Build_DataGroupList()
		{
			Groups.Clear();
			bool num = PGgroupsForPropCompon(_selectedProperty);
			if (num)
			{
				CurveList.SetCurves(Groups.ToArray());
				if (Groups.Count == 1)
				{
					CurveList.IsCheckAll = true;
					return num;
				}
				CurveList.IsCheckAll = false;
			}
			return num;
		}

		private bool PGgroupsForPropCompon(string sProp)
		{
			int count = PropGroups.Count;
			for (int i = 0; i < count; i++)
			{
				string[] array = ((PlotwizardHelper)_help).ParseLine(PropGroups[i]);
				if (array[0] == sProp && !Groups.Contains(array[1]))
				{
					Groups.Add(array[1]);
				}
			}
			return Groups.Count > 0;
		}

		private void DetermineMoleType()
		{
			if (MT_Present("MOLEFRAC"))
			{
				MoleType = WpfPLRegressionResources.MoleFraction_AxisTitle;
			}
			else if (MT_Present("MOLEPCT"))
			{
				MoleType = WpfPLRegressionResources.MolePercent_AxisTitle;
			}
			else if (MT_Present("MASSFRAC"))
			{
				MoleType = WpfPLRegressionResources.MassFraction_AxisTitle;
			}
			else if (MT_Present("MASSPCT"))
			{
				MoleType = WpfPLRegressionResources.MassPercent_AxisTitle;
			}
			else
			{
				MoleType = DetermineMoleTypeViaLine6();
			}
		}

		private bool MT_Present(string mtype)
		{
			string path = "Output/" + mtype;
			return _context.FindNode(path).Elements.Count != 0;
		}

		private string DetermineMoleTypeViaLine6()
		{
			string result = "";
			IHNode iHNode = _context.FindNode("Output/LINE1");
			IHNode iHNode2 = _context.FindNode("Output/LINE6");
			int count = iHNode.Elements.Count;
			for (int i = 0; i < count; i++)
			{
				string text = string.Empty;
				if (iHNode.Elements[i, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].Elements.Count > 0)
				{
					text = (dynamic)iHNode.Elements[i, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].Elements[0, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].get_Value(Type.Missing);
				}
				if (text != "TEMP" && text != "PRESSURE")
				{
					if (iHNode2.Elements[i, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].Elements.Count > 0)
					{
						text = (dynamic)iHNode2.Elements[i, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].Elements[0, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].get_Value(Type.Missing);
					}
					return text switch
					{
						"MOLEFRAC" => WpfPLRegressionResources.MoleFraction_AxisTitle, 
						"MOLEPCT" => WpfPLRegressionResources.MolePercent_AxisTitle, 
						"MASSFRAC" => WpfPLRegressionResources.MassFraction_AxisTitle, 
						"MASSPCT" => WpfPLRegressionResources.MassPercent_AxisTitle, 
						_ => text, 
					};
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		private string PressureAmtUnits(string GroupID)
		{
			if (_bAnalysis)
			{
				return AmtUnits(Pres[0, 1], GroupID);
			}
			return AmtUnits(Pres[0, 0], GroupID);
		}

		private string TemperatureAmtUnits(string GroupID)
		{
			if (_bAnalysis)
			{
				return AmtUnits(Temp[0, 1], GroupID);
			}
			return AmtUnits(Temp[0, 0], GroupID);
		}

		private string AmtUnits(int utype, string GroupID)
		{
			string text = string.Empty;
			if (utype >= 0)
			{
				string path = VarNames[1] + "/Prop Data/PROFILE/" + GroupID;
				if (_bAnalysis)
				{
					path = VarNames[1] + "/Prop Data/PROFILE2/" + GroupID;
				}
				IHNode iHNode = _context.FindNode(path);
				text = ((dynamic)iHNode.Elements[utype, 1, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].get_Value(Type.Missing)).ToString();
				string text2 = ((dynamic)iHNode.Elements[utype, -7, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].get_Value(Type.Missing)).ToString();
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
				{
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text2))
					{
						text = text + " " + text2;
					}
				}
				else if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text2))
				{
					text = text2;
				}
			}
			return text;
		}

		private void AppendExtraProps()
		{
			int count = Groups.Count;
			for (int i = 0; i < count; i++)
			{
				IHNode iHNode = _context.FindNode(VarNames[0] + "/" + Groups[i]);
				if (_bAnalysis)
				{
					iHNode = _context.FindNode(VarNames[8] + "/" + Groups[i]);
				}
				int count2 = iHNode.Elements.Count;
				for (int j = 0; j < count2; j++)
				{
					string text = (dynamic)iHNode.Elements[j, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].get_Value(Type.Missing);
					string[] array = ((PlotwizardHelper)_help).ParseLine(text);
					if (array != null && array.Count() > 0 && array[0] == "PROP" && !XPropLabels.Contains(array[1]))
					{
						XPropLabels.Add(array[1]);
					}
				}
			}
		}

		private void Build_Node_Lookup()
		{
			int count = Groups.Count;
			int count2 = Compons.Count;
			Temp = new int[count, 2];
			Pres = new int[count, 2];
			Xc = new int[count, 2, count2];
			X2c = new int[count, 2, count2];
			Yc = new int[count, 2, count2];
			YXc = new int[count, 2, count2];
			XProps = new int[count, XPropLabels.Count, 2, count2];
			for (int i = 0; i < count; i++)
			{
				for (int j = 0; j < 2; j++)
				{
					Temp[i, j] = -1;
					Pres[i, j] = -1;
					for (int k = 0; k < count2; k++)
					{
						Xc[i, j, k] = -1;
						X2c[i, j, k] = -1;
						Yc[i, j, k] = -1;
						YXc[i, j, k] = -1;
						for (int l = 0; l < XPropLabels.Count; l++)
						{
							XProps[i, l, j, k] = -1;
							if (k == count2)
							{
								XProps[i, l, j, count2] = -1;
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			int num = 0;
			for (int m = 0; m < count; m++)
			{
				IHNode iHNode = _context.FindNode(VarNames[0] + "/" + Groups[m]);
				int count3 = iHNode.Elements.Count;
				for (int n = 0; n < count3; n++)
				{
					string text = (dynamic)iHNode.Elements[n, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].get_Value(Type.Missing);
					string[] array = ((PlotwizardHelper)_help).ParseLine(text);
					if (array == null || array.Count() <= 0)
					{
						continue;
					}
					switch (array[0])
					{
					case "T":
						Temp[m, 0] = int.Parse(array[1]) - 1;
						if (int.Parse(array[2]) == 2)
						{
							Temp[m, 1] = int.Parse(array[1]);
						}
						break;
					case "P":
						Pres[m, 0] = int.Parse(array[1]) - 1;
						if (int.Parse(array[2]) == 2)
						{
							Pres[m, 1] = int.Parse(array[1]);
						}
						break;
					case "X":
						num = Compons.IndexOf(array[1]);
						if (num >= 0)
						{
							Xc[m, 0, num] = int.Parse(array[2]) - 1;
							if (int.Parse(array[3]) == 2)
							{
								Xc[m, 1, num] = int.Parse(array[2]);
							}
						}
						break;
					case "X2":
						num = Compons.IndexOf(array[1]);
						if (num >= 0)
						{
							X2c[m, 0, num] = int.Parse(array[2]) - 1;
							if (int.Parse(array[3]) == 2)
							{
								X2c[m, 1, num] = int.Parse(array[2]);
							}
						}
						break;
					case "Y":
						num = Compons.IndexOf(array[1]);
						if (num >= 0)
						{
							Yc[m, 0, num] = int.Parse(array[2]) - 1;
							if (int.Parse(array[3]) == 2)
							{
								Yc[m, 1, num] = int.Parse(array[2]);
							}
						}
						break;
					case "Y-X":
						num = Compons.IndexOf(array[1]);
						if (num >= 0)
						{
							YXc[m, 0, num] = int.Parse(array[2]) - 1;
							if (int.Parse(array[3]) == 2)
							{
								YXc[m, 1, num] = int.Parse(array[2]);
							}
						}
						break;
					case "PROP":
					{
						num = Compons.IndexOf(array[2]);
						List<string> list = new List<string>(XPropLabels);
						if (num >= 0)
						{
							int num2 = list.IndexOf(array[1]);
							if (XGroups[m, num2])
							{
								XProps[m, num2, 0, num] = int.Parse(array[3]) - 1;
								if (int.Parse(array[4]) == 2)
								{
									XProps[m, num2, 1, num] = int.Parse(array[3]);
								}
							}
						}
						else
						{
							if (!(array[2] == "ALL"))
							{
								break;
							}
							int num3 = list.IndexOf(array[1]);
							if (num3 == -1)
							{
								break;
							}
							for (int num4 = 0; num4 < Compons.Count; num4++)
							{
								XProps[m, num3, 0, num4] = int.Parse(array[3]) - 1;
								if (int.Parse(array[4]) == 2)
								{
									XProps[m, num3, 1, num4] = int.Parse(array[3]);
								}
							}
						}
						break;
					}
					}
				}
				if (!_bAnalysis)
				{
					continue;
				}
				iHNode = _context.FindNode(VarNames[8] + "/" + Groups[m]);
				if (iHNode == null)
				{
					break;
				}
				count3 = iHNode.Elements.Count;
				for (int num5 = 0; num5 < count3; num5++)
				{
					string text2 = (dynamic)iHNode.Elements[num5, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].get_Value(Type.Missing);
					string[] array2 = ((PlotwizardHelper)_help).ParseLine(text2);
					if (array2 == null || array2.Count() <= 0)
					{
						continue;
					}
					switch (array2[0])
					{
					case "T":
						if (int.Parse(array2[2]) == 1)
						{
							Temp[m, 1] = int.Parse(array2[1]) - 1;
						}
						break;
					case "P":
						if (int.Parse(array2[2]) == 1)
						{
							Pres[m, 1] = int.Parse(array2[1]) - 1;
						}
						break;
					case "X":
						num = Compons.IndexOf(array2[1]);
						if (num >= 0 && int.Parse(array2[3]) == 1)
						{
							Xc[m, 1, num] = int.Parse(array2[2]) - 1;
						}
						break;
					case "X2":
						num = Compons.IndexOf(array2[1]);
						if (num >= 0 && int.Parse(array2[3]) == 1)
						{
							X2c[m, 1, num] = int.Parse(array2[2]) - 1;
						}
						break;
					case "Y":
						num = Compons.IndexOf(array2[1]);
						if (num >= 0 && int.Parse(array2[3]) == 1)
						{
							Yc[m, 1, num] = int.Parse(array2[2]) - 1;
						}
						break;
					case "Y-X":
						num = Compons.IndexOf(array2[1]);
						if (num >= 0 && int.Parse(array2[3]) == 1)
						{
							YXc[m, 1, num] = int.Parse(array2[2]) - 1;
						}
						break;
					case "PROP":
						num = Compons.IndexOf(array2[2]);
						if (num >= 0)
						{
							int num6 = XPropLabels.IndexOf(array2[1]);
							if (num6 != -1 && XGroups[m, num6] && int.Parse(array2[4]) == 1)
							{
								XProps[m, num6, 1, num] = int.Parse(array2[3]) - 1;
							}
						}
						else
						{
							if (!(array2[2] == "ALL"))
							{
								break;
							}
							int num7 = XPropLabels.IndexOf(array2[1]);
							if (num7 == -1 || num7 > XGroups.GetUpperBound(1) + 1)
							{
								break;
							}
							for (int num8 = 0; num8 < Compons.Count; num8++)
							{
								if (int.Parse(array2[4]) == 1)
								{
									XProps[m, num7, 1, num8] = int.Parse(array2[3]) - 1;
								}
							}
						}
						break;
					}
				}
			}
		}

		private string Join(string[] xW, string sFill, bool bTrail)
		{
			return ((PlotwizardHelper)_help).Join(xW, sFill, bTrail);
		}

		private void ExecuteOKCommand(object sender, ExecutedRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (AnaResultRadio.IsChecked == true)
			{
				_bAnalysis = true;
			}
			else
			{
				_bAnalysis = false;
			}
			UpdateWizardData();
			((PlotwizardHelper)_help).CloseForm((UserControl)this, (bool?)true);
		}

		private void CanExecuteOKCommand(object sender, CanExecuteRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			int num = CurveList.GetSelectedCurves().Count();
			if (num == CurveList.Curves.Count)
			{
				CurveList.IsCheckAll = true;
			}
			else
			{
				string[] selectedCurves = CurveList.GetSelectedCurves();
				CurveList.IsCheckAll = false;
				CurveList.SetSelectedCurves(selectedCurves);
			}
			e.CanExecute = num != 0;
		}

		private void ExecuteCancelCommand(object sender, ExecutedRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			((PlotwizardHelper)_help).CloseForm((UserControl)this, (bool?)false);
		}

		private string SaveConfigValue()
		{
			_ = string.Empty;
			StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
			stringBuilder.Append("Property=");
			stringBuilder.Append(_selectedProperty);
			stringBuilder.Append(";");
			stringBuilder.Append("RadioButton=");
			stringBuilder.Append(_bAnalysis);
			stringBuilder.Append(";");
			stringBuilder.Append("AnalRadioButtonEnable=");
			bool isEnabled = AnaResultRadio.IsEnabled;
			stringBuilder.Append(isEnabled);
			stringBuilder.Append(";");
			stringBuilder.Append("DataGroup=");
			ObservableCollection<AesCheckListBoxItem> observableCollection = new ObservableCollection<AesCheckListBoxItem>(CurveList.Curves);
			int count = observableCollection.Count;
			for (int i = 0; i < count; i++)
			{
				if (i > 0)
				{
					stringBuilder.Append(",");
				}
				string text = observableCollection[i].Text;
				stringBuilder.Append(text);
			}
			stringBuilder.Append(";");
			stringBuilder.Append("SelectedDataGroup=");
			List<string> list = new List<string>(CurveList.GetSelectedCurves());
			int count2 = list.Count;
			for (int j = 0; j < count2; j++)
			{
				if (j > 0)
				{
					stringBuilder.Append(",");
				}
				string value = list[j];
				stringBuilder.Append(value);
			}
			stringBuilder.Append(";");
			return stringBuilder.ToString();
		}

		private void SetCheckedValues(string ConfigString)
		{
			string[] array = ConfigString.Split(';');
			int num = 0;
			string selectedProperty = array[num].Substring(array[num].IndexOf('=') + 1);
			SelectedProperty = selectedProperty;
			num++;
			selectedProperty = array[num].Substring(array[num].IndexOf('=') + 1);
			if (selectedProperty.Equals("true", StringComparison.CurrentCultureIgnoreCase))
			{
				AnaResultRadio.IsChecked = true;
			}
			else
			{
				RegResultRadio.IsChecked = true;
			}
			num++;
			selectedProperty = array[num].Substring(array[num].IndexOf('=') + 1);
			if (selectedProperty.Equals("true", StringComparison.CurrentCultureIgnoreCase))
			{
				AnaResultRadio.IsEnabled = true;
			}
			else
			{
				AnaResultRadio.IsEnabled = false;
				RegResultRadio.IsChecked = true;
			}
			num++;
			selectedProperty = array[num].Substring(array[num].IndexOf('=') + 1);
			string[] array2 = selectedProperty.Split(',');
			List<string> list = new List<string>();
			string[] array3 = array2;
			foreach (string item in array3)
			{
				list.Add(item);
			}
			CurveList.SetCurves(list.ToArray());
			num++;
			selectedProperty = array[num].Substring(array[num].IndexOf('=') + 1);
			string[] array4 = selectedProperty.Split(',');
			list = new List<string>();
			array3 = array4;
			foreach (string item2 in array3)
			{
				list.Add(item2);
			}
			CurveList.SetSelectedCurves(list.ToArray());
		}

			//IL_0047: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)

		void IRibbonPlotWizardType.Context(object HNode, ref object HWizardPlot, ref int ierror)
		{
			if (HWizardPlot == null || HNode == null)
			{
				ierror = 1;
				return;
			}
			_context = HNode as IHNode;
			_wizardPlot = HWizardPlot as IHWizardPlot;
			if (!BuildFullSet())
			{
				ierror = 1;
				return;
			}
			bool flag = false;
			IHNode iHNode = _context.FindNode("Output/LINE9");
			if (iHNode == null || iHNode.Elements == null || iHNode.Elements.Count < 1)
			{
				AnaResultRadio.IsEnabled = false;
				RegResultRadio.IsChecked = true;
				flag = true;
			}
			IHNode iHNode2 = _context.FindNode("Input/ANALYSIS");
			if (iHNode2 != null && !flag)
			{
				if ((dynamic)iHNode2.get_Value(Type.Missing) == "YES")
				{
					AnaResultRadio.IsChecked = true;
				}
				else
				{
					RegResultRadio.IsChecked = true;
				}
			}
			BuildListAvailDataObj();
			Build_cboBasisList(ListAvailBasis[0]);
			Build_cboPropertyList(ListAvailProperty[0]);
			Build_cboComponList(ListAvailCompon[0]);
			Build_lstDataGroupList();
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_currentConfigValue))
			{
				RestoreConfigValue(_currentConfigValue);
			}
		}

		public void UpdateWizardData()
		{
			MoleType = _selectedBasis;
			Groups = CurveList.GetSelectedCurves().ToList();
			AppendExtraProps();
			XGroups = new bool[Groups.Count, XPropLabels.Count];
			int num = new List<string>(XPropLabels).IndexOf(_selectedProperty);
			for (int i = 0; i < Groups.Count; i++)
			{
				for (int j = 0; j < XPropLabels.Count; j++)
				{
					if (j == num)
					{
						XGroups[i, j] = true;
					}
					else
					{
						XGroups[i, j] = false;
					}
				}
			}
			int count = Groups.Count;
			IHNode[] array = new IHNode[count];
			IHNode[] array2 = new IHNode[count];
			for (int k = 0; k < count; k++)
			{
				array[k] = _context.FindNode(VarNames[1] + "/Prop Data/PROFILE/" + Groups[k]);
				if (array[k] == null || array[k].Elements.Count == 0)
				{
					return;
				}
				if (_bAnalysis)
				{
					array2[k] = _context.FindNode(VarNames[1] + "/Prop Data/PROFILE2/" + Groups[k]);
					if (array2[k] == null || array2[k].Elements.Count == 0)
					{
						return;
					}
				}
			}
			Build_Node_Lookup();
			int num2 = Compons.IndexOf(_component);
			int num3 = 0;
			int num4 = 0;
			bool flag = false;
			for (int l = 0; l < count; l++)
			{
				for (int m = 0; m < Compons.Count; m++)
				{
					if (XProps[l, num, 0, m] != -1 && Xc[l, 0, m] != -1)
					{
						num3++;
						int num5 = array[l].Elements.get_RowCount(1);
						if (num5 > num4)
						{
							num4 = num5;
						}
						if (m == Compons.Count)
						{
							flag = true;
						}
					}
					if (XProps[l, num, 1, m] != -1 && (Xc[l, 0, m] != -1 || Xc[l, 1, m] != -1))
					{
						num3++;
						int num6 = array[l].Elements.get_RowCount(1);
						if (num6 > num4)
						{
							num4 = num6;
						}
						if (_bAnalysis)
						{
							num6 = array2[l].Elements.get_RowCount(1);
							if (num6 > num4)
							{
								num4 = num6;
							}
						}
						if (m == Compons.Count)
						{
							flag = true;
						}
					}
					if (flag)
					{
						break;
					}
				}
			}
			if (num3 == 0 || num4 == 0)
			{
				((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_YAxisTitle(Type.Missing, _selectedProperty);
				_wizardPlot.PlotVal.XAxisTitle = _selectedBasis + " " + _component;
				_wizardPlot.RemoveAllDependentVariable();
				return;
			}
			_wizardPlot.RemoveAllDependentVariable();
			_wizardPlot.PlotVal.NVariables = num3;
			_wizardPlot.PlotVal.NXVariables = num3;
			_wizardPlot.PlotVal.NPoints = num4;
			int num7 = 0;
			bool[] array3 = new bool[num3];
			for (int n = 0; n < num3; n++)
			{
				array3[n] = false;
			}
			for (int num8 = 0; num8 < count; num8++)
			{
				try
				{
					int num9 = array[num8].Elements.get_RowCount(1);
					int num10 = array[num8].Elements.get_RowCount(1);
					if (_bAnalysis)
					{
						num10 = array2[num8].Elements.get_RowCount(1);
					}
					for (int num11 = 0; num11 < Compons.Count; num11++)
					{
						if (num7 >= num3)
						{
							break;
						}
						if (XProps[num8, num, 0, num11] != -1 && Xc[num8, 0, num11] != -1)
						{
							for (int num12 = 0; num12 < num9; num12++)
							{
								if (!array3[num7])
								{
									string arg = (flag ? "" : Compons[num11]);
									((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_Label(num7, string.Format(WpfPLRegressionResources.GroupCompUnit_Exp_F_F_ID_Legend, arg, Groups[num8]));
									((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_CurveStyle(num7, 15);
									array3[num7] = true;
								}
								if (Xc[num8, 0, num2] >= 0)
								{
									((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_XData(num7, num12, (short)1, (object)array[num8].Elements[Xc[num8, 0, num2], num12, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing]);
								}
								else
								{
									((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_XData(num7, num12, (short)1, (object)array[num8].Elements[Xc[num8, 0, num11], num12, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing]);
								}
								((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_YData(num7, num12, (short)1, (object)array[num8].Elements[XProps[num8, num, 0, num11], num12, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing]);
							}
							num7++;
						}
						if (XProps[num8, num, 1, num11] == -1 || (Xc[num8, 0, num11] == -1 && Xc[num8, 1, num11] == -1))
						{
							continue;
						}
						int num13 = ((Xc[num8, 1, num2] != -1) ? 1 : 0);
						int num14 = num9;
						if (_bAnalysis && num13 == 1)
						{
							num14 = num10;
						}
						for (int num15 = 0; num15 < num14; num15++)
						{
							if (!array3[num7])
							{
								string text = (flag ? "" : Compons[num11]);
								((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_Label(num7, string.Format(WpfPLRegressionResources.GroupCompUnit_Est_F_ID_Legend, text + " " + Groups[num8]));
								((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_CurveStyle(num7, 240);
								array3[num7] = true;
							}
							if (_bAnalysis && num13 == 1)
							{
								if (Xc[num8, num13, num2] >= 0)
								{
									((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_XData(num7, num15, (short)1, (object)array2[num8].Elements[Xc[num8, num13, num2], num15, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing]);
								}
								else
								{
									((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_XData(num7, num15, (short)1, (object)array2[num8].Elements[Xc[num8, num13, num11], num15, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing]);
								}
								((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_YData(num7, num15, (short)1, (object)array2[num8].Elements[XProps[num8, num, 1, num11], num15, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing]);
							}
							else
							{
								if (Xc[num8, num13, num2] >= 0)
								{
									((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_XData(num7, num15, (short)1, (object)array[num8].Elements[Xc[num8, num13, num2], num15, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing]);
								}
								else
								{
									((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_XData(num7, num15, (short)1, (object)array[num8].Elements[Xc[num8, num13, num11], num15, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing]);
								}
								((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_YData(num7, num15, (short)1, (object)array[num8].Elements[XProps[num8, num, 1, num11], num15, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing]);
							}
						}
						num7++;
					}
				}
				catch
				{
				}
			}
			string unitString = ((PlotwizardHelper)_help).GetUnitString(_context.Application.Tree, _wizardPlot);
			((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_YAxisTitle(Type.Missing, _selectedProperty + " " + unitString);
			_wizardPlot.PlotVal.XAxisTitle = _selectedBasis;
			_currentConfigValue = SaveConfigValue();
		}

		private bool BuildFullSet()
		{
			FullSet.Clear();
			IHNode iHNode = _context.FindNode(VarNames[0]);
			if (_bAnalysis)
			{
				iHNode = _context.FindNode(VarNames[8]);
			}
			if (iHNode == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			int count = iHNode.Elements.Count;
			if (count == 0)
			{
				return false;
			}
			bool flag = false;
			for (int i = 0; i < count; i++)
			{
				string text = iHNode.Elements.get_Label(0, i, Type.Missing);
				int basisIdxForDataset = _help.GetBasisIdxForDataset(_context, text);
				string text2 = basisIdxForDataset.ToString();
				if (basisIdxForDataset < 0)
				{
					continue;
				}
				string text3 = (dynamic)iHNode.Elements[i, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].Elements["1", Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].get_Value(Type.Missing);
				text3 = text3.Trim();
				if (!(text3 != ""))
				{
					continue;
				}
				StringWork.Clear();
				StringWork2.Clear();
				string[] array = ((PlotwizardHelper)_help).ParseLine(text3);
				for (int j = 0; j < array.Length; j++)
				{
					int result = 0;
					if (!int.TryParse(array[j], out result) || result != 8)
					{
						continue;
					}
					flag = true;
					string path = "Output/LINE7/" + text;
					IHNode iHNode2 = _context.FindNode(path);
					if (iHNode2 == null)
					{
						break;
					}
					int count2 = iHNode2.Elements.Count;
					for (int k = 2; k <= count2; k++)
					{
						string loc_or_name = $"{k:####0}";
						string text4 = (dynamic)iHNode2.Elements[loc_or_name, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].get_Value(Type.Missing);
						string[] array2 = ((PlotwizardHelper)_help).ParseLine(text4);
						switch (array2[0])
						{
						case "X":
						case "X2":
						case "Y":
						case "Y-X":
						{
							string text6 = array2[1].Trim();
							if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text6) && !StringWork.Contains(text6))
							{
								StringWork.Add(text6);
							}
							break;
						}
						case "PROP":
						{
							string text5 = array2[1].Trim();
							if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text5) && !StringWork2.Contains(text5))
							{
								StringWork2.Add(text5);
							}
							break;
						}
						}
					}
					StringWork.Sort();
					StringWork2.Sort();
					path = Join(StringWork.ToArray(), " ", bTrail: false);
					for (int l = 0; l < StringWork2.Count; l++)
					{
						string item = text + Tabchr + text2 + Tabchr + StringWork2[l] + Tabchr + path;
						if (!FullSet.Contains(item))
						{
							FullSet.Add(item);
						}
					}
					break;
				}
			}
			if (!flag)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (!BuildListAvailBasis())
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (!BuildBasisSubset(ListAvailBasis[0]))
			{
				return false;
			}
			return true;
		}

		private bool BuildListAvailBasis()
		{
			ListAvailBasis.Clear();
			BasisSubset.Clear();
			StringWork.Clear();
			for (int i = 0; i < FullSet.Count; i++)
			{
				string[] array = FullSet[i].Split(Tabchr.ToCharArray());
				string text = array[1].Trim();
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text) && !StringWork.Contains(text))
				{
					StringWork.Add(array[1]);
				}
			}
			StringWork.Sort();
			for (int j = 0; j < StringWork.Count; j++)
			{
				ListAvailBasis.Add(StringWork[j]);
			}
			return ListAvailBasis.Count > 0;
		}

		private bool BuildBasisSubset(string SelBasis)
		{
			BasisSubset.Clear();
			for (int i = 0; i < FullSet.Count; i++)
			{
				if (FullSet[i].Split(Tabchr.ToCharArray())[1].Trim() == SelBasis)
				{
					BasisSubset.Add(FullSet[i]);
				}
			}
			if (BasisSubset.Count == 0)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (!BuildListAvailProperty())
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (!BuildPropertySubset(ListAvailProperty[0]))
			{
				return false;
			}
			return true;
		}

		private bool BuildListAvailProperty()
		{
			_ = BasisSubset.Count;
			ListAvailProperty.Clear();
			PropertySubset.Clear();
			StringWork.Clear();
			for (int i = 0; i < BasisSubset.Count; i++)
			{
				string text = BasisSubset[i].Split(Tabchr.ToCharArray())[2].Trim();
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text) && !StringWork.Contains(text))
				{
					StringWork.Add(text);
				}
			}
			StringWork.Sort();
			for (int j = 0; j < StringWork.Count; j++)
			{
				ListAvailProperty.Add(StringWork[j]);
			}
			return ListAvailProperty.Count > 0;
		}

		private bool BuildPropertySubset(string SelProperty)
		{
			PropertySubset.Clear();
			for (int i = 0; i < BasisSubset.Count; i++)
			{
				if (BasisSubset[i].Split(Tabchr.ToCharArray())[2].Trim() == SelProperty)
				{
					PropertySubset.Add(BasisSubset[i]);
				}
			}
			if (PropertySubset.Count == 0)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (!BuildListAvailCompon())
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (!BuildComponSubset(ListAvailCompon[0]))
			{
				return false;
			}
			return true;
		}

		private bool BuildListAvailCompon()
		{
			ListAvailCompon.Clear();
			ComponSubset.Clear();
			StringWork.Clear();
			for (int i = 0; i < PropertySubset.Count; i++)
			{
				string[] array = PropertySubset[i].Split(Tabchr.ToCharArray())[3].Split(' ');
				for (int j = 0; j < array.Length; j++)
				{
					string text = array[j].Trim();
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text) && !StringWork.Contains(text))
					{
						StringWork.Add(array[j]);
					}
				}
			}
			StringWork.Sort();
			for (int k = 0; k < StringWork.Count; k++)
			{
				ListAvailCompon.Add(StringWork[k]);
			}
			return ListAvailCompon.Count > 0;
		}

		private bool BuildComponSubset(string SelCompon)
		{
			ComponSubset.Clear();
			for (int i = 0; i < PropertySubset.Count; i++)
			{
				string[] array = PropertySubset[i].Split(Tabchr.ToCharArray())[3].Split(' ');
				for (int j = 0; j < array.Length; j++)
				{
					if (array[j].Trim() == SelCompon)
					{
						ComponSubset.Add(PropertySubset[i]);
						break;
					}
				}
			}
			return ComponSubset.Count > 0;
		}

		private bool BuildListAvailDataObj()
		{
			ListAvailDataobj.Clear();
			for (int i = 0; i < PropertySubset.Count; i++)
			{
				string[] array = PropertySubset[i].Split(Tabchr.ToCharArray());
				ListAvailDataobj.Add(array[0]);
			}
			return ListAvailDataobj.Count > 0;
		}

		private void Build_cboBasisList(string SelBasis)
		{
			BaseTypes.Clear();
			foreach (string item in ListAvailBasis)
			{
				BaseTypes.Add(BasisString[int.Parse(item)]);
			}
			SelectedBasis = BasisString[int.Parse(SelBasis)];
		}

		private void Build_cboPropertyList(string SelProperty)
		{
			PropertyTypes.Clear();
			foreach (string item in ListAvailProperty)
			{
				PropertyTypes.Add(item);
			}
			SelectedProperty = SelProperty;
		}

		private void Build_cboComponList(string SelCompon)
		{
			Compons = new List<string>(ListAvailCompon);
			_component = SelCompon;
		}

		private void Build_lstDataGroupList()
		{
			CurveList.SetCurves(ListAvailDataobj.ToArray());
			if (ListAvailDataobj.Count == 1)
			{
				CurveList.IsCheckAll = true;
			}
			else
			{
				CurveList.IsCheckAll = false;
			}
		}

		private void DetermineMoleType()
		{
			if (MT_Present("MOLEFRAC"))
			{
				MoleType = WpfPLRegressionResources.MoleFraction_AxisTitle;
			}
			else if (MT_Present("MOLEPCT"))
			{
				MoleType = WpfPLRegressionResources.MolePercent_AxisTitle;
			}
			else if (MT_Present("MASSFRAC"))
			{
				MoleType = WpfPLRegressionResources.MassFraction_AxisTitle;
			}
			else if (MT_Present("MASSPCT"))
			{
				MoleType = WpfPLRegressionResources.MassPercent_AxisTitle;
			}
			else
			{
				MoleType = DetermineMoleTypeViaLine6();
			}
		}

		private bool MT_Present(string mtype)
		{
			string path = "Output/" + mtype;
			return _context.FindNode(path).Elements.Count != 0;
		}

		private string DetermineMoleTypeViaLine6()
		{
			string result = "";
			IHNode iHNode = _context.FindNode("Output/LINE1");
			IHNode iHNode2 = _context.FindNode("Output/LINE6");
			int count = iHNode.Elements.Count;
			for (int i = 0; i < count; i++)
			{
				string text = string.Empty;
				if (iHNode.Elements[i, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].Elements.Count > 0)
				{
					text = (dynamic)iHNode.Elements[i, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].Elements[0, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].get_Value(Type.Missing);
				}
				if (text != "TEMP" && text != "PRESSURE")
				{
					if (iHNode2.Elements[i, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].Elements.Count > 0)
					{
						text = (dynamic)iHNode2.Elements[i, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].Elements[0, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].get_Value(Type.Missing);
					}
					return text switch
					{
						"MOLEFRAC" => WpfPLRegressionResources.MoleFraction_AxisTitle, 
						"MOLEPCT" => WpfPLRegressionResources.MolePercent_AxisTitle, 
						"MASSFRAC" => WpfPLRegressionResources.MassFraction_AxisTitle, 
						"MASSPCT" => WpfPLRegressionResources.MassPercent_AxisTitle, 
						_ => text, 
					};
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		private string PressureAmtUnits(string GroupID)
		{
			if (_bAnalysis)
			{
				return AmtUnits(Pres[0, 1], GroupID);
			}
			return AmtUnits(Pres[0, 0], GroupID);
		}

		private string TemperatureAmtUnits(string GroupID)
		{
			if (_bAnalysis)
			{
				return AmtUnits(Temp[0, 1], GroupID);
			}
			return AmtUnits(Temp[0, 0], GroupID);
		}

		private string AmtUnits(int utype, string GroupID)
		{
			string path = VarNames[1] + "/Prop Data/PROFILE/" + GroupID;
			if (_bAnalysis)
			{
				path = VarNames[1] + "/Prop Data/PROFILE2/" + GroupID;
			}
			IHNode iHNode = _context.FindNode(path);
			string text = (dynamic)iHNode.Elements[utype, 1, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].get_Value(Type.Missing);
			string text2 = (dynamic)iHNode.Elements[utype, -7, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].get_Value(Type.Missing);
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
			{
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text2))
				{
					text = text + " " + text2;
				}
			}
			else if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text2))
			{
				text = text2;
			}
			return text;
		}

		private void Build_Node_Lookup()
		{
			int count = Groups.Count;
			int count2 = Compons.Count;
			Temp = new int[count, 2];
			Pres = new int[count, 2];
			Xc = new int[count, 2, count2];
			X2c = new int[count, 2, count2];
			Yc = new int[count, 2, count2];
			YXc = new int[count, 2, count2];
			XProps = new int[count, XPropLabels.Count, 2, count2 + 1];
			for (int i = 0; i < count; i++)
			{
				for (int j = 0; j < 2; j++)
				{
					Temp[i, j] = -1;
					Pres[i, j] = -1;
					for (int k = 0; k < count2; k++)
					{
						Xc[i, j, k] = -1;
						X2c[i, j, k] = -1;
						Yc[i, j, k] = -1;
						YXc[i, j, k] = -1;
						for (int l = 0; l < XPropLabels.Count; l++)
						{
							XProps[i, l, j, k] = -1;
							if (k == count2 - 1)
							{
								XProps[i, l, j, count2] = -1;
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			for (int m = 0; m < count; m++)
			{
				IHNode iHNode = _context.FindNode(VarNames[0] + "/" + Groups[m]);
				int count3 = iHNode.Elements.Count;
				for (int n = 0; n < count3; n++)
				{
					string text = (dynamic)iHNode.Elements[n, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].get_Value(Type.Missing);
					string[] array = ((PlotwizardHelper)_help).ParseLine(text);
					int num = 0;
					switch (array[0])
					{
					case "T":
						Temp[m, 0] = int.Parse(array[1]) - 1;
						if (int.Parse(array[2]) == 2)
						{
							Temp[m, 1] = int.Parse(array[1]);
						}
						break;
					case "P":
						Pres[m, 0] = int.Parse(array[1]) - 1;
						if (int.Parse(array[2]) == 2)
						{
							Pres[m, 1] = int.Parse(array[1]);
						}
						break;
					case "X":
						num = Compons.IndexOf(array[1]);
						if (num >= 0)
						{
							Xc[m, 0, num] = int.Parse(array[2]) - 1;
							if (int.Parse(array[3]) == 2)
							{
								Xc[m, 1, num] = int.Parse(array[2]);
							}
						}
						break;
					case "X2":
						num = Compons.IndexOf(array[1]);
						if (num >= 0)
						{
							X2c[m, 0, num] = int.Parse(array[2]) - 1;
							if (int.Parse(array[3]) == 2)
							{
								X2c[m, 1, num] = int.Parse(array[2]);
							}
						}
						break;
					case "Y":
						num = Compons.IndexOf(array[1]);
						if (num >= 0)
						{
							Yc[m, 0, num] = int.Parse(array[2]) - 1;
							if (int.Parse(array[3]) == 2)
							{
								Yc[m, 1, num] = int.Parse(array[2]);
							}
						}
						break;
					case "Y-X":
						num = Compons.IndexOf(array[1]);
						if (num >= 0)
						{
							YXc[m, 0, num] = int.Parse(array[2]) - 1;
							if (int.Parse(array[3]) == 2)
							{
								YXc[m, 1, num] = int.Parse(array[2]);
							}
						}
						break;
					case "PROP":
						num = Compons.IndexOf(array[2]);
						if (num >= 0)
						{
							int num2 = XPropLabels.IndexOf(array[1]);
							if (num2 != -1 && XGroups[m, num2])
							{
								XProps[m, num2, 0, num] = int.Parse(array[3]) - 1;
								if (int.Parse(array[4]) == 2)
								{
									XProps[m, num2, 1, num] = int.Parse(array[3]);
								}
							}
						}
						else
						{
							if (!(array[2] == "ALL"))
							{
								break;
							}
							int num3 = XPropLabels.IndexOf(array[1]);
							if (num3 == -1 || num3 > XGroups.GetUpperBound(1) + 1)
							{
								break;
							}
							for (int num4 = 0; num4 <= Compons.Count; num4++)
							{
								XProps[m, num3, 0, num4] = int.Parse(array[3]) - 1;
								if (int.Parse(array[4]) == 2)
								{
									XProps[m, num3, 1, num4] = int.Parse(array[3]);
								}
							}
						}
						break;
					}
				}
				if (!_bAnalysis)
				{
					continue;
				}
				iHNode = _context.FindNode(VarNames[8] + "/" + Groups[m]);
				if (iHNode == null)
				{
					break;
				}
				count3 = iHNode.Elements.Count;
				for (int num5 = 0; num5 < count3; num5++)
				{
					string text2 = (dynamic)iHNode.Elements[num5, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].get_Value(Type.Missing);
					string[] array2 = ((PlotwizardHelper)_help).ParseLine(text2);
					int num6 = 0;
					if (array2 == null)
					{
						break;
					}
					switch (array2[0])
					{
					case "T":
						if (int.Parse(array2[2]) == 1)
						{
							Temp[m, 1] = int.Parse(array2[1]) - 1;
						}
						break;
					case "P":
						if (int.Parse(array2[2]) == 1)
						{
							Pres[m, 1] = int.Parse(array2[1]) - 1;
						}
						break;
					case "X":
						num6 = Compons.IndexOf(array2[1]);
						if (num6 >= 0 && int.Parse(array2[3]) == 1)
						{
							Xc[m, 1, num6] = int.Parse(array2[2]) - 1;
						}
						break;
					case "X2":
						num6 = Compons.IndexOf(array2[1]);
						if (num6 >= 0 && int.Parse(array2[3]) == 1)
						{
							X2c[m, 1, num6] = int.Parse(array2[2]) - 1;
						}
						break;
					case "Y":
						num6 = Compons.IndexOf(array2[1]);
						if (num6 >= 0 && int.Parse(array2[3]) == 1)
						{
							Yc[m, 1, num6] = int.Parse(array2[2]) - 1;
						}
						break;
					case "Y-X":
						num6 = Compons.IndexOf(array2[1]);
						if (num6 >= 0 && int.Parse(array2[3]) == 1)
						{
							YXc[m, 1, num6] = int.Parse(array2[2]) - 1;
						}
						break;
					case "PROP":
						num6 = Compons.IndexOf(array2[2]);
						if (num6 >= 0)
						{
							int num7 = XPropLabels.IndexOf(array2[1]);
							if (num7 != -1 && XGroups[m, num7] && int.Parse(array2[4]) == 1)
							{
								XProps[m, num7, 1, num6] = int.Parse(array2[3]) - 1;
							}
						}
						else
						{
							if (!(array2[2] == "ALL"))
							{
								break;
							}
							int num8 = XPropLabels.IndexOf(array2[1]);
							if (num8 == -1 || num8 > XGroups.GetUpperBound(1) + 1)
							{
								break;
							}
							for (int num9 = 0; num9 <= Compons.Count; num9++)
							{
								if (int.Parse(array2[4]) == 1)
								{
									XProps[m, num8, 1, num9] = int.Parse(array2[3]) - 1;
								}
							}
						}
						break;
					}
				}
			}
		}

		private void AppendExtraProps()
		{
			XPropLabels = new List<string>(new string[5] { "KVL", "ALPHA", "GAMMA", "TBUB", "PBUB" });
			int count = Groups.Count;
			for (int i = 0; i < count; i++)
			{
				IHNode iHNode = _context.FindNode(VarNames[0] + "/" + Groups[i]);
				if (_bAnalysis)
				{
					iHNode = _context.FindNode(VarNames[8] + "/" + Groups[i]);
				}
				int count2 = iHNode.Elements.Count;
				for (int j = 0; j < count2; j++)
				{
					string text = (dynamic)iHNode.Elements[j, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].get_Value(Type.Missing);
					string[] array = ((PlotwizardHelper)_help).ParseLine(text);
					if (array[0] == "PROP" && !XPropLabels.Contains(array[1]))
					{
						XPropLabels.Add(array[1]);
					}
				}
			}
		}

		private string Join(string[] xW, string sFill, bool bTrail)
		{
			return ((PlotwizardHelper)_help).Join(xW, sFill, bTrail);
		}

		private string SaveConfigValue()
		{
			_ = string.Empty;
			StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
			stringBuilder.Append("Basis=");
			stringBuilder.Append(_selectedBasis);
			stringBuilder.Append(";");
			stringBuilder.Append("Property=");
			stringBuilder.Append(_selectedProperty);
			stringBuilder.Append(";");
			stringBuilder.Append("RadioButton=");
			stringBuilder.Append(_bAnalysis);
			stringBuilder.Append(";");
			stringBuilder.Append("AnalRadioButtonEnable=");
			bool isEnabled = AnaResultRadio.IsEnabled;
			stringBuilder.Append(isEnabled);
			stringBuilder.Append(";");
			stringBuilder.Append("DataGroup=");
			ObservableCollection<AesCheckListBoxItem> observableCollection = new ObservableCollection<AesCheckListBoxItem>(CurveList.Curves);
			int count = observableCollection.Count;
			for (int i = 0; i < count; i++)
			{
				if (i > 0)
				{
					stringBuilder.Append(",");
				}
				string text = observableCollection[i].Text;
				stringBuilder.Append(text);
			}
			stringBuilder.Append(";");
			stringBuilder.Append("SelectedDataGroup=");
			List<string> list = new List<string>(CurveList.GetSelectedCurves());
			int count2 = list.Count;
			for (int j = 0; j < count2; j++)
			{
				if (j > 0)
				{
					stringBuilder.Append(",");
				}
				string value = list[j];
				stringBuilder.Append(value);
			}
			stringBuilder.Append(";");
			return stringBuilder.ToString();
		}

		private void RestoreConfigValue(string ConfigString)
		{
			string[] array = ConfigString.Split(';');
			int num = 0;
			string selectedBasis = array[num].Substring(array[num].IndexOf('=') + 1);
			SelectedBasis = selectedBasis;
			num++;
			selectedBasis = array[num].Substring(array[num].IndexOf('=') + 1);
			SelectedProperty = selectedBasis;
			num++;
			selectedBasis = array[num].Substring(array[num].IndexOf('=') + 1);
			if (selectedBasis.Equals("true", StringComparison.CurrentCultureIgnoreCase))
			{
				AnaResultRadio.IsChecked = true;
			}
			else
			{
				RegResultRadio.IsChecked = true;
			}
			num++;
			selectedBasis = array[num].Substring(array[num].IndexOf('=') + 1);
			if (selectedBasis.Equals("true", StringComparison.CurrentCultureIgnoreCase))
			{
				AnaResultRadio.IsEnabled = true;
			}
			else
			{
				AnaResultRadio.IsEnabled = false;
				RegResultRadio.IsChecked = true;
			}
			num++;
			selectedBasis = array[num].Substring(array[num].IndexOf('=') + 1);
			string[] array2 = selectedBasis.Split(',');
			List<string> list = new List<string>();
			string[] array3 = array2;
			foreach (string item in array3)
			{
				list.Add(item);
			}
			CurveList.SetCurves(list.ToArray());
			num++;
			selectedBasis = array[num].Substring(array[num].IndexOf('=') + 1);
			string[] array4 = selectedBasis.Split(',');
			list = new List<string>();
			array3 = array4;
			foreach (string item2 in array3)
			{
				list.Add(item2);
			}
			CurveList.SetSelectedCurves(list.ToArray());
		}

		private void ChangePropertyType()
		{
			if (BuildPropertySubset(_selectedProperty))
			{
				BuildListAvailDataObj();
				Build_lstDataGroupList();
			}
		}

		private void ExecuteOKCommand(object sender, ExecutedRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (AnaResultRadio.IsChecked == true)
			{
				_bAnalysis = true;
			}
			else
			{
				_bAnalysis = false;
			}
			UpdateWizardData();
			((PlotwizardHelper)_help).CloseForm((UserControl)this, (bool?)true);
		}

		private void CanExecuteOKCommand(object sender, CanExecuteRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			int num = CurveList.GetSelectedCurves().Count();
			if (num == CurveList.Curves.Count)
			{
				CurveList.IsCheckAll = true;
			}
			else
			{
				string[] selectedCurves = CurveList.GetSelectedCurves();
				CurveList.IsCheckAll = false;
				CurveList.SetSelectedCurves(selectedCurves);
			}
			e.CanExecute = num != 0;
		}

		private void ExecuteCancelCommand(object sender, ExecutedRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			((PlotwizardHelper)_help).CloseForm((UserControl)this, (bool?)false);
		}

			minWidth = 300.0;
		}

			//IL_0047: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)

			}

	public class RegressionHelp : PlotwizardHelper
	{
		public RegressionHelp(string fileName, long fileId)
			: base(fileName, fileId)
		{
		}

		public void BuildComponentList(IHNode top, IHWizardPlot wizardPlot, List<string> listComps)
		{
			if (listComps == null)
			{
				listComps = new List<string>();
			}
			else
			{
				listComps.Clear();
			}
			IHNode iHNode = top.FindNode("Output/LINE1");
			IHNode iHNode2 = top.FindNode("Output/LINE3");
			int count = iHNode.Elements.Count;
			for (int i = 0; i < count; i++)
			{
				string text = string.Empty;
				if (iHNode.Elements[i, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].Elements.Count > 0)
				{
					text = (dynamic)iHNode.Elements[i, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].Elements[0, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].get_Value(Type.Missing);
				}
				if (text != "TEMP" && text != "PRESSURE")
				{
					if (iHNode2.Elements[i, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].Elements.Count > 0)
					{
						text = (dynamic)iHNode2.Elements[i, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].Elements[0, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].get_Value(Type.Missing);
					}
					if (!listComps.Contains(text) && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
					{
						listComps.Add(text);
					}
				}
			}
			listComps.Sort();
		}

		public int GetBasisIdxForDataset(IHNode top, string dataset)
		{
			IHNode iHNode = top.FindNode("Output/LINE1");
			IHNode iHNode2 = top.FindNode("Output/LINE6");
			int num = -1;
			int count = iHNode.Elements.Count;
			for (int i = 0; i < count; i++)
			{
				string text = string.Empty;
				string text2 = string.Empty;
				if (iHNode.Elements[i, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].Elements.Count > 0)
				{
					text = (dynamic)iHNode.Elements[i, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].Elements[0, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].get_Value(Type.Missing);
					text2 = iHNode.Elements[i, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].Elements[0, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].get_Name(Type.Missing);
				}
				if (text2 == dataset && text != "TEMP" && text != "PRESSURE")
				{
					switch ((dynamic)iHNode2.Elements[i, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].Elements[0, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].get_Value(Type.Missing))
					{
					case "MASSFRAC":
						num = 0;
						break;
					case "MASSPCT":
					case "MASSPERCENT":
						num = 1;
						break;
					case "MOLEFRAC":
						num = 2;
						break;
					case "MOLEPCT":
					case "MOLEPERCENT":
						num = 3;
						break;
					default:
						continue;
					}
					break;
				}
			}
			if (num == -1)
			{
				bool flag = false;
				for (int j = 0; j <= 3; j++)
				{
					if (flag)
					{
						break;
					}
					IHNode iHNode3 = null;
					switch (j)
					{
					case 0:
						iHNode3 = top.FindNode("Output/MASSFRAC");
						break;
					case 1:
						iHNode3 = top.FindNode("Output/MASSPCT");
						break;
					case 2:
						iHNode3 = top.FindNode("Output/MOLEFRAC");
						break;
					case 3:
						iHNode3 = top.FindNode("Output/MOLEPCT");
						break;
					}
					if (iHNode3 == null)
					{
						continue;
					}
					int count2 = iHNode3.Elements.Count;
					for (int k = 0; k < count2; k++)
					{
						if (iHNode3.Elements[k, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].get_Name(Type.Missing) == dataset)
						{
							num = j;
							flag = true;
							break;
						}
					}
				}
			}
			if (num == -1)
			{
				num = 2;
			}
			return num;
		}

		public int GetSelectedBasisIdx(string sBasis)
		{
			int result = 0;
			if (sBasis == WpfPLRegressionResources.MassFraction_AxisTitle)
			{
				result = 0;
			}
			else if (sBasis == WpfPLRegressionResources.MassPercent_AxisTitle)
			{
				result = 1;
			}
			else if (sBasis == WpfPLRegressionResources.MoleFraction_AxisTitle)
			{
				result = 2;
			}
			else if (sBasis == WpfPLRegressionResources.MolePercent_AxisTitle)
			{
				result = 3;
			}
			return result;
		}

		public object GetValidXYData(dynamic value)
		{
			if (!((value == null) ? true : false))
			{
				return value;
			}
			return 0;
		}
	}

		public ResidualS3(string type)
		{
			//IL_000c: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0016: Expected O, but got Unknown
			InitializeComponent();
			CommandBinding commandBinding = new CommandBinding(OKCommand, ExecuteOKCommand);
			CommandBinding commandBinding2 = new CommandBinding(CancelCommand, ExecuteCancelCommand);
			base.CommandBindings.Add(commandBinding);
			base.CommandBindings.Add(commandBinding2);
			_bHasInputDialog = true;
			_type = type;
			if (_imgIcon == null)
			{
				Assembly.GetExecutingAssembly();
