				if (_type == "Residual")
				{
					name = "WpfPLRegression.Images.regressvs2.ico";
				}
				Stream manifestResourceStream = Assembly.GetExecutingAssembly().GetManifestResourceStream(name);
				_imgIcon = new Bitmap(manifestResourceStream);
			}
			base.DataContext = this;
		}

		void IRibbonPlotWizardType.Context(object HNode, ref object HWizardPlot, ref int ierror)
		{
			if (HWizardPlot == null || HNode == null)
			{
				ierror = 1;
				return;
			}
			SetVarNames();
			_context = HNode as IHNode;
			_wizardPlot = HWizardPlot as IHWizardPlot;
			if (!Build_DataGroupList())
			{
				ierror = 1;
			}
			else if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_currentConfigValue))
			{
				RestoreConfigValue(_currentConfigValue);
			}
		}

		public void UpdateWizardData()
		{
			if (VariableList.Count <= 0)
			{
				_wizardPlot.set_ErrorStatus(3, true);
				return;
			}
			IHNode iHNode = _context.FindNode(VarNames[2]);
			IHNode iHNode2 = _context.FindNode(VarNames[3]);
			IHNode iHNode3 = _context.FindNode(VarNames[4]);
			IHNode iHNode4 = _context.FindNode(VarNames[5]);
			IHNode iHNode5 = _context.FindNode(VarNames[6]);
			_context.FindNode(VarNames[8]);
			int num = 0;
			int num2 = 0;
			int basis = _wizardPlot.basis;
			if ((uint)(basis - 12) <= 1u)
			{
				if (iHNode3 == null || iHNode3.Elements.Count == 0)
				{
					return;
				}
				int index = GroupVars.IndexOf(_selectedDataGroup + SelectedVariable);
				SelectedItemIdx = Indices[index];
				if (CompPropUnit.Count != 0)
				{
					SelectedItemUnit = CompPropUnit[index];
				}
				num2 = iHNode3.Elements[SelectedItemIdx, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].Elements[0, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].Elements.Count;
				num++;
				_wizardPlot.RemoveAllDependentVariable();
				_wizardPlot.PlotVal.NVariables = num;
				_wizardPlot.PlotVal.NXVariables = num;
				_wizardPlot.PlotVal.NPoints = num2;
				int index2 = 0;
				int num3 = 0;
				bool flag = false;
				switch (_wizardPlot.basis)
				{
				case 12:
				{
					if (iHNode3 == null || iHNode3.Elements.Count == 0 || iHNode4 == null || iHNode4.Elements.Count == 0)
					{
						return;
					}
					num3 = iHNode3.Elements[SelectedItemIdx, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].Elements[0, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].Elements.Count;
					for (int j = 0; j < num3; j++)
					{
						if (!flag)
						{
							string label = SelectedDataGroup + " %ID%";
							((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_Label(index2, label);
							((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_CurveStyle(index2, 15);
							flag = true;
						}
						((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_XData(index2, j, (short)1, (object)iHNode3.Elements[SelectedItemIdx, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].Elements[0, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].Elements[j, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing]);
						((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_YData(index2, j, (short)1, (object)iHNode4.Elements[SelectedItemIdx, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].Elements[0, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].Elements[j, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing]);
					}
					_wizardPlot.PlotVal.SquarePlot = true;
					_wizardPlot.PlotVal.ShowDiagonalLine = true;
					break;
				}
				case 13:
				{
					if (iHNode3 == null || iHNode3.Elements.Count == 0 || iHNode == null || iHNode.Elements.Count == 0 || iHNode2 == null || iHNode2.Elements.Count == 0 || iHNode5 == null || iHNode5.Elements.Count == 0)
					{
						return;
					}
					num3 = iHNode3.Elements[SelectedItemIdx, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].Elements[0, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].Elements.Count;
					for (int i = 0; i < num3; i++)
					{
						if (!flag)
						{
							string text = (dynamic)iHNode.Elements[SelectedItemIdx, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].Elements[0, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].get_Value(Type.Missing);
							text = ((text == "TEMP") ? string.Format(WpfPLRegressionResources.F_Temperature_F_ID, SelectedDataGroup, SelectedItemUnit) : ((!(text == "PRESSURE")) ? ((string)(SelectedDataGroup + " " + (dynamic)iHNode2.Elements[SelectedItemIdx, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].Elements[0, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].get_Value(Type.Missing) + " %ID%")) : string.Format(WpfPLRegressionResources.F_Pressure_F_ID, SelectedDataGroup, SelectedItemUnit)));
							((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_Label(index2, text);
							((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_CurveStyle(index2, 15);
							flag = true;
						}
						((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_XData(index2, i, (short)1, (object)iHNode3.Elements[SelectedItemIdx, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].Elements[0, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].Elements[i, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing]);
						((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_YData(index2, i, (short)1, (object)iHNode5.Elements[SelectedItemIdx, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].Elements[0, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].Elements[i, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing]);
					}
					_wizardPlot.PlotVal.ShowZeroLine = true;
					break;
				}
				}
				SetAxisLabels();
				_currentConfigValue = SaveConfigValue();
			}
			else
			{
				_wizardPlot.set_ErrorStatus(3, true);
			}
		}

		private void ExecuteOKCommand(object sender, ExecutedRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			UpdateWizardData();
			_help.CloseForm((UserControl)this, (bool?)true);
		}

		private void ExecuteCancelCommand(object sender, ExecutedRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			_help.CloseForm((UserControl)this, (bool?)false);
		}

		private void SetVarNames()
		{
			VarNames = new string[9] { "Output/LINE7", "Output", "Output/LINE1", "Output/LINE3", "Output/ESTVALUE", "Output/EXP_VALUE", "Output/DIFF", "Output/LINE5", "Output/LINE6" };
		}

		private bool Build_DataGroupList()
		{
			if (!Build_DataGroupVariableList())
			{
				return false;
			}
			SelectedDataGroup = DataGroupList[0];
			return true;
		}

		private bool Build_DataGroupVariableList()
		{
			IHNode iHNode = _context.FindNode(VarNames[2]);
			IHNode iHNode2 = _context.FindNode(VarNames[3]);
			IHNode iHNode3 = _context.FindNode(VarNames[4]);
			IHNode iHNode4 = _context.FindNode(VarNames[7]);
			IHNode iHNode5 = _context.FindNode(VarNames[8]);
			if (iHNode == null || iHNode2 == null || iHNode3 == null || iHNode4 == null || iHNode5 == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			int count = iHNode.Elements.Count;
			if (count == 0)
			{
				return false;
			}
			bool result = false;
			for (int i = 0; i < count; i++)
			{
				int num = 0;
				if (iHNode3.Elements[i, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].Elements.Count > 0)
				{
					num = iHNode3.Elements[i, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].Elements[0, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].Elements.Count;
				}
				if (num > 0)
				{
					result = true;
					string sGroup = iHNode.Elements[i, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].Elements.get_Label(0, 0, Type.Missing);
					string sProp = (dynamic)iHNode.Elements[i, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].Elements[0, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].get_Value(Type.Missing);
					string sComp = (dynamic)iHNode2.Elements[i, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].Elements[0, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].get_Value(Type.Missing);
					string sPhase = (dynamic)iHNode4.Elements[i, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].Elements[0, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].get_Value(Type.Missing);
					string sUnit = (dynamic)iHNode5.Elements[i, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].Elements[0, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].get_Value(Type.Missing);
					AddGroupVars(sGroup, sProp, sComp, sPhase, sUnit, i);
					AddGroup(sGroup);
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		private void AddGroupVars(string sGroup, string sProp, string sComp, string sPhase, string sUnit, int nIdx)
		{
			string item = sGroup + "   " + sProp;
			if (sProp != "TEMP" && sProp != "PRESSURE")
			{
				item = sGroup + "   " + sProp + "   " + sComp + "   " + sPhase;
			}
			if (!GroupVars.Contains(item))
			{
				GroupVars.Add(item);
				Indices.Add(nIdx);
				CompPropUnit.Add(sUnit);
			}
		}

		private void AddGroup(string sGroup)
		{
			if (!DataGroupList.Contains(sGroup))
			{
				DataGroupList.Add(sGroup);
			}
		}

		private bool MT_Present(string mtype)
		{
			string path = "Output/" + mtype;
			return _context.FindNode(path).Elements.Count != 0;
		}

		private void Build_VariableList()
		{
			ObservableCollection<string> observableCollection = new ObservableCollection<string>();
			for (int i = 0; i < GroupVars.Count; i++)
			{
				if (SelectedDataGroup == GroupVars[i].Substring(0, SelectedDataGroup.Length))
				{
					string text = GroupVars[i].Substring(SelectedDataGroup.Length);
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text.Trim()))
					{
						observableCollection.Add(text);
					}
				}
			}
			VariableList = observableCollection;
			SelectedVariable = observableCollection[0];
		}

		private void SetAxisLabels()
		{
			string text = SelectedVariable.Trim();
			string[] array = _help.ParseLine(text);
			string text2 = null;
			if (SelectedItemUnit != null)
			{
				text2 = SelectedItemUnit;
			}
			switch (_wizardPlot.basis)
			{
			case 12:
			{
				string text3 = array[0];
				if (!(text3 == "TEMP"))
				{
					if (text3 == "PRESSURE")
					{
						((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_YAxisTitle(Type.Missing, string.Format(WpfPLRegressionResources.ResidualS3_Est_Pres_YAxisTitle, text2));
						_wizardPlot.PlotVal.XAxisTitle = string.Format(WpfPLRegressionResources.ResidualS3_Exp_Pres_XAxisTitle, text2);
					}
					else if (IsBasis(text2))
					{
						string[] xW2 = Slice(text.Split(' '), 1, text.Split(' ').Length - 1);
						((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_YAxisTitle(Type.Missing, string.Format(WpfPLRegressionResources.ResidualS3_Est_YAxisTitle, text2, Join(xW2, " ", bTrail: true)));
						_wizardPlot.PlotVal.XAxisTitle = string.Format(WpfPLRegressionResources.ResidualS3_Exp_XAxisTitle, text2, Join(xW2, " ", bTrail: true));
					}
					else
					{
						string text5 = null;
						text5 = ((text2 == null) ? " " : ", ");
						((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_YAxisTitle(Type.Missing, string.Format(WpfPLRegressionResources.ResidualS3_Est0_YAxisTitle, array[0] + text5 + text2));
						_wizardPlot.PlotVal.XAxisTitle = string.Format(WpfPLRegressionResources.ResidualS3_Exp0_XAxisTitle, array[0] + text5 + text2);
					}
				}
				else
				{
					((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_YAxisTitle(Type.Missing, string.Format(WpfPLRegressionResources.ResidualS3_Est_Temp_YAxisTitle, text2));
					_wizardPlot.PlotVal.XAxisTitle = string.Format(WpfPLRegressionResources.ResidualS3_Exp_Temp_XAxisTitle, text2);
				}
				break;
			}
			case 13:
			{
				string text3 = array[0];
				if (!(text3 == "TEMP"))
				{
					if (text3 == "PRESSURE")
					{
						((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_YAxisTitle(Type.Missing, string.Format(WpfPLRegressionResources.ResidualS3_Res_Pres_YAxisTitle, text2));
						_wizardPlot.PlotVal.XAxisTitle = string.Format(WpfPLRegressionResources.ResidualS3_Exp_Pres_XAxisTitle, text2);
					}
					else if (IsBasis(text2))
					{
						string[] xW = Slice(text.Split(' '), 1, text.Split(' ').Length - 1);
						((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_YAxisTitle(Type.Missing, string.Format(WpfPLRegressionResources.ResidualS3_Res_YAxisTitle, text2, Join(xW, " ", bTrail: false)));
						_wizardPlot.PlotVal.XAxisTitle = string.Format(WpfPLRegressionResources.ResidualS3_Exp_XAxisTitle, text2, Join(xW, " ", bTrail: false));
					}
					else
					{
						string text4 = null;
						text4 = ((text2 == null) ? " " : ", ");
						((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_YAxisTitle(Type.Missing, string.Format(WpfPLRegressionResources.ResidualS3_Res0_YAxisTitle, array[0] + text4 + text2));
						_wizardPlot.PlotVal.XAxisTitle = string.Format(WpfPLRegressionResources.ResidualS3_Exp0_XAxisTitle, array[0] + text4 + text2);
					}
				}
				else
				{
					((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_YAxisTitle(Type.Missing, string.Format(WpfPLRegressionResources.ResidualS3_Res_Temp_YAxisTitle, text2));
					_wizardPlot.PlotVal.XAxisTitle = string.Format(WpfPLRegressionResources.ResidualS3_Exp_Temp_XAxisTitle, text2);
				}
				break;
			}
			}
		}

		private bool IsBasis(string sVal)
		{
			if (sVal == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			switch (sVal.ToUpper())
			{
			case "MASSFRAC":
			case "MOLEFRAC":
			case "MASSFRACTION":
			case "MOLEFRACTION":
			case "MASSPCT":
			case "MOLEPCT":
			case "MASSPERCENT":
			case "MOLEPERCENT":
				return true;
			default:
				return false;
			}
		}

		private string[] Slice(string[] xW, int nLow, int nHigh)
		{
			if (xW == null)
			{
				return xW;
			}
			List<string> list = new List<string>();
			int num = nHigh - nLow + 1;
			for (int i = 0; i < num && nLow + i < xW.Length; i++)
			{
				list.Add(xW[nLow + i]);
			}
			return list.ToArray();
		}

		private string Join(string[] xW, string sFill, bool bTrail)
		{
			if (xW == null || xW.Length == 0)
			{
				return string.Empty;
			}
			string text = "";
			for (int i = 0; i < xW.Length; i++)
			{
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(xW[i]))
				{
					text += xW[i];
					if (i != xW.Length - 1 || bTrail)
					{
						text += sFill;
					}
				}
				else if (i == xW.Length - 1 && bTrail && text.Length == 0)
				{
					text = sFill;
				}
			}
			return text;
		}

		private string SaveConfigValue()
		{
			_ = string.Empty;
			StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
			stringBuilder.Append("DataGroup=");
			stringBuilder.Append(SelectedDataGroup);
			stringBuilder.Append(";");
			stringBuilder.Append("Variable=");
			stringBuilder.Append(SelectedVariable);
			stringBuilder.Append(";");
			return stringBuilder.ToString();
		}

		private void RestoreConfigValue(string ConfigString)
		{
			string[] array = ConfigString.Split(';');
			int num = 0;
			string selectedDataGroup = array[num].Substring(array[num].IndexOf('=') + 1);
			SelectedDataGroup = selectedDataGroup;
			num++;
			selectedDataGroup = array[num].Substring(array[num].IndexOf('=') + 1);
			SelectedVariable = selectedDataGroup;
		}

		private void FirePropertyChangedEvent(string propName)
		{
			if (this.PropertyChanged != null)
			{
				this.PropertyChanged(this, new PropertyChangedEventArgs(propName));
			}
		}

		}

			{
				caption = WpfPLRegressionResources.ResidualCaption;
			}
			else
			{
				caption = WpfPLRegressionResources.EstvsExp;
			}
		}

		}

		{
			defaultWidth = 350.0;
		}

		{
			minHeight = 300.0;
		}

		void IPlotWizardTemplate.SetMinWidth(ref double minWidth)
		{
			minWidth = 400.0;
		}

		public ResidualS3_new(string type)
		{
			//IL_000c: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0016: Expected O, but got Unknown
			InitializeComponent();
			CommandBinding commandBinding = new CommandBinding(OKCommand, ExecuteOKCommand, CanExecuteOKCommand);
			CommandBinding commandBinding2 = new CommandBinding(CancelCommand, ExecuteCancelCommand);
			base.CommandBindings.Add(commandBinding);
			base.CommandBindings.Add(commandBinding2);
			_bHasInputDialog = true;
			_type = type;
			if (_imgIcon == null)
			{
				Assembly.GetExecutingAssembly();
				string name = "WpfPLRegression.Images.regressvs1.ico";
				if (_type == "Residual")
				{
					name = "WpfPLRegression.Images.regressvs2.ico";
				}
				Stream manifestResourceStream = Assembly.GetExecutingAssembly().GetManifestResourceStream(name);
				_imgIcon = new Bitmap(manifestResourceStream);
			}
			CurveList.Header = WpfPLRegressionResources.SelectDataGrou_EstvsExp;
			base.DataContext = this;
		}

		void IRibbonPlotWizardType.Context(object HNode, ref object HWizardPlot, ref int ierror)
		{
			if (HWizardPlot == null || HNode == null)
			{
				ierror = 1;
				return;
			}
			SetVarNames();
			_context = HNode as IHNode;
			_wizardPlot = HWizardPlot as IHWizardPlot;
			if (!Build_DataGroupList())
			{
				ierror = 1;
			}
			else if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_currentConfigValue))
			{
				RestoreConfigValue(_currentConfigValue);
			}
		}

		private IEnumerable<string> RestoreCurves(string ConfigString, List<ResidualItem> items)
		{
			string[] array = ConfigString.Split(';');
			string[] array2 = array[1].Substring(array[1].IndexOf('=') + 1).Split(',');
			string[] array3 = array2;
			foreach (string curve in array3)
			{
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(curve) && items.FirstOrDefault((ResidualItem p) => p.DataSetName.Equals(curve)) != null)
				{
					yield return curve;
				}
			}
		}

		public void UpdateWizardData()
		{
			Build_DataGroupList();
			if (VariableList.Count <= 0)
			{
				_wizardPlot.set_ErrorStatus(3, true);
			}
			else
			{
				if (!groupDic.ContainsKey(SelectedVariable))
				{
					return;
				}
				List<ResidualItem> list = groupDic[SelectedVariable];
				_context.FindNode(VarNames[2]);
				_context.FindNode(VarNames[3]);
				IHNode iHNode = _context.FindNode(VarNames[4]);
				IHNode iHNode2 = _context.FindNode(VarNames[5]);
				_context.FindNode(VarNames[6]);
				IHNode iHNode3 = _context.FindNode(VarNames[8]);
				List<string> dataSetList = new List<string>();
				foreach (AesCheckListBoxItem c in CurveList.Curves)
				{
					if (c.IsChecked && list.FirstOrDefault((ResidualItem p) => p.DataSetName.Equals(c.Text)) != null)
					{
						dataSetList.Add(c.Text);
					}
					c.IsChecked = false;
				}
				_help.CloseForm((UserControl)this, (bool?)true);
				if (dataSetList.Count < 1 && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(_currentConfigValue))
				{
					dataSetList = RestoreCurves(_currentConfigValue, list).ToList();
				}
				_wizardPlot.RemoveAllDependentVariable();
				_wizardPlot.PlotVal.NPoints = 0;
				_wizardPlot.PlotVal.NVariables = dataSetList.Count;
				_wizardPlot.PlotVal.NXVariables = dataSetList.Count;
				_wizardPlot.PlotVal.LineStyle = 1;
				_wizardPlot.PlotVal.ShowDiagonalLine = true;
				if (dataSetList.Count == 1)
				{
					_wizardPlot.PlotVal.SquarePlot = true;
				}
				else
				{
					_wizardPlot.PlotVal.SquarePlot = false;
				}
				int num = 0;
				SelectedItemUnit = string.Empty;
				int i;
				for (i = 0; i < dataSetList.Count; i++)
				{
					int startIndex = list.FirstOrDefault((ResidualItem p) => p.DataSetName.Equals(dataSetList[i])).StartIndex;
					if (iHNode == null || iHNode.Elements.Count == 0 || iHNode2 == null || iHNode2.Elements.Count == 0)
					{
						return;
					}
					if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(SelectedItemUnit))
					{
						SelectedItemUnit = (dynamic)iHNode3.Elements[startIndex, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].Elements[0, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].get_Value(Type.Missing);
					}
					num = iHNode.Elements[startIndex, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].Elements[0, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].Elements.Count;
					if (dataSetList.Count == 1)
					{
						_wizardPlot.PlotVal.NPoints = num;
					}
					else
					{
						_wizardPlot.PlotVal.NPoints = ((_wizardPlot.PlotVal.NPoints > num) ? _wizardPlot.PlotVal.NPoints : num);
					}
					string text = SelectedVariable + " %ID%";
					((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_Label(i, dataSetList[i] + " " + text);
					((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_CurveStyle(i, 15);
					for (int num2 = 0; num2 < num; num2++)
					{
						((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_XData(i, num2, (short)1, (object)iHNode.Elements[startIndex, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].Elements[0, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].Elements[num2, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing]);
						((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_YData(i, num2, (short)1, (object)iHNode2.Elements[startIndex, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].Elements[0, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].Elements[num2, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing]);
					}
				}
				SetAxisLabels();
				_wizardPlot.PlotVal.Title = WpfPLRegressionResources.EstvsExp;
				_currentConfigValue = SaveConfigValue(dataSetList);
			}
		}

		private void ExecuteOKCommand(object sender, ExecutedRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			UpdateWizardData();
			_help.CloseForm((UserControl)this, (bool?)true);
		}

		private void CanExecuteOKCommand(object sender, CanExecuteRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			int num = CurveList.GetSelectedCurves().Count();
			if (num == CurveList.Curves.Count)
			{
				CurveList.IsCheckAll = true;
			}
			else
			{
				string[] selectedCurves = CurveList.GetSelectedCurves();
				CurveList.IsCheckAll = false;
				CurveList.SetSelectedCurves(selectedCurves);
			}
			e.CanExecute = num != 0;
		}

		private void ExecuteCancelCommand(object sender, ExecutedRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			_help.CloseForm((UserControl)this, (bool?)false);
		}

		private void SetVarNames()
		{
			VarNames = new string[9] { "Output/LINE7", "Output", "Output/LINE1", "Output/LINE3", "Output/ESTVALUE", "Output/EXP_VALUE", "Output/DIFF", "Output/LINE5", "Output/LINE6" };
		}

		private bool Build_DataGroupList()
		{
			if (!Build_DataGroupVariableList())
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(SelectedVariable))
			{
				SelectedVariable = VariableList[0];
			}
			return true;
		}

		private bool Build_DataGroupVariableList()
		{
			groupDic.Clear();
			List<string> list = new List<string>();
			IHNode iHNode = _context.FindNode(VarNames[2]);
			IHNode iHNode2 = _context.FindNode(VarNames[3]);
			IHNode iHNode3 = _context.FindNode(VarNames[4]);
			IHNode iHNode4 = _context.FindNode(VarNames[7]);
			if (iHNode == null || iHNode2 == null || iHNode3 == null || iHNode4 == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			int count = iHNode.Elements.Count;
			if (count == 0)
			{
				return false;
			}
			bool result = false;
			for (int i = 0; i < count; i++)
			{
				int num = 0;
				if (iHNode3.Elements[i, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].Elements.Count > 0)
				{
					num = iHNode3.Elements[i, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].Elements[0, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].Elements.Count;
				}
				if (num > 0)
				{
					result = true;
					string dataSetName = iHNode.Elements[i, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].Elements.get_Label(0, 0, Type.Missing);
					string text = (dynamic)iHNode.Elements[i, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].Elements[0, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].get_Value(Type.Missing);
					string arg = (dynamic)iHNode2.Elements[i, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].Elements[0, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].get_Value(Type.Missing);
					string arg2 = (dynamic)iHNode4.Elements[i, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].Elements[0, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].get_Value(Type.Missing);
					string text2 = text;
					if (text != "TEMP" && text != "PRESSURE")
					{
						text2 = $"{text} {arg} {arg2}";
					}
					if (!groupDic.Keys.Contains(text2))
					{
						groupDic.Add(text2, new List<ResidualItem>
						{
							new ResidualItem
							{
								DataSetName = dataSetName,
								StartIndex = i
							}
						});
						list.Add(text2);
					}
					else
					{
						groupDic[text2].Add(new ResidualItem
						{
							DataSetName = dataSetName,
							StartIndex = i
						});
					}
				}
			}
			list.Sort();
			foreach (string item in list)
			{
				if (!VariableList.Contains(item))
				{
					VariableList.Add(item);
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		private void Build_VariableList(string selectVal)
		{
			List<string> list = new List<string>();
			if (!groupDic.ContainsKey(selectVal))
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (ResidualItem item in groupDic[selectVal])
			{
				list.Add(item.DataSetName);
			}
			CurveList.SetCurves(list.ToArray());
		}

		private bool IsBasis(string sVal)
		{
			if (sVal == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			switch (sVal.ToUpper())
			{
			case "MASSFRAC":
			case "MOLEFRAC":
			case "MASSFRACTION":
			case "MOLEFRACTION":
			case "MASSPCT":
			case "MOLEPCT":
			case "MASSPERCENT":
			case "MOLEPERCENT":
				return true;
			default:
				return false;
			}
		}

		private void SetAxisLabels()
		{
			string text = SelectedVariable.Trim();
			string[] array = _help.ParseLine(text);
			string text2 = null;
			if (SelectedItemUnit != null)
			{
				text2 = SelectedItemUnit;
			}
			string text3 = array[0];
			if (!(text3 == "TEMP"))
			{
				if (text3 == "PRESSURE")
				{
					((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_YAxisTitle(Type.Missing, string.Format(WpfPLRegressionResources.ResidualS3_Est_Pres_YAxisTitle, text2));
					_wizardPlot.PlotVal.XAxisTitle = string.Format(WpfPLRegressionResources.ResidualS3_Exp_Pres_XAxisTitle, text2);
					return;
				}
				if (IsBasis(text2))
				{
					((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_YAxisTitle(Type.Missing, string.Format(WpfPLRegressionResources.ResidualS3_Est_YAxisTitle, text2, text));
					_wizardPlot.PlotVal.XAxisTitle = string.Format(WpfPLRegressionResources.ResidualS3_Exp_XAxisTitle, text2, text);
					return;
				}
				string text4 = null;
				text4 = ((text2 == null) ? " " : ", ");
				((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_YAxisTitle(Type.Missing, string.Format(WpfPLRegressionResources.ResidualS3_Est0_YAxisTitle, text + text4 + text2));
				_wizardPlot.PlotVal.XAxisTitle = string.Format(WpfPLRegressionResources.ResidualS3_Exp0_XAxisTitle, text + text4 + text2);
			}
			else
			{
				((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_YAxisTitle(Type.Missing, string.Format(WpfPLRegressionResources.ResidualS3_Est_Temp_YAxisTitle, text2));
				_wizardPlot.PlotVal.XAxisTitle = string.Format(WpfPLRegressionResources.ResidualS3_Exp_Temp_XAxisTitle, text2);
			}
		}

		private string SaveConfigValue(IEnumerable<string> selectedDataSetList)
		{
			_ = string.Empty;
			StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
			stringBuilder.Append("Variable =");
			stringBuilder.Append(SelectedVariable);
			stringBuilder.Append(";");
			stringBuilder.Append("SelectedCurves =");
			foreach (string selectedDataSet in selectedDataSetList)
			{
				stringBuilder.Append(selectedDataSet);
				stringBuilder.Append(",");
			}
			return stringBuilder.ToString();
		}

		private void RestoreConfigValue(string ConfigString)
		{
			string[] array = ConfigString.Split(';');
			string selectedVariable = array[0].Substring(array[0].IndexOf('=') + 1);
			SelectedVariable = selectedVariable;
		}

			if (this.PropertyChanged != null)
			{
				this.PropertyChanged(this, new PropertyChangedEventArgs(propName));
			}
		}

		void IRibbonPlotWizardType.Context(object HNode, ref object HWizardPlot, ref int ierror)
		{
			if (HWizardPlot == null || HNode == null)
			{
				ierror = 1;
				return;
			}
			SetVarNames();
			_context = HNode as IHNode;
			_wizardPlot = HWizardPlot as IHWizardPlot;
			if (!Build_CandidateList())
			{
				ierror = 1;
				return;
			}
			DetermineMoleType();
			Build_ComponentList(SelectedDataGroup);
			if (Compons != null && Compons.Count > 0)
			{
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(_component1))
				{
					_component1 = MoleType + " " + Compons[0];
				}
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(_component2))
				{
					_component2 = Compons[1];
				}
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(_component3))
				{
					_component3 = Compons[2];
				}
			}
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(_currentConfigValue))
			{
				ATEXAxisTitle.Text = _component1;
				ATEYAxisTitle.Text = _component2;
				ATEZAxisTitle.Text = _component3;
			}
			else
			{
				RestoreConfigValue(_currentConfigValue);
			}
		}

		public void UpdateWizardData()
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(SelectedDataGroup))
			{
				_wizardPlot.set_ErrorStatus(3, true);
				return;
			}
			IHNode iHNode = _context.FindNode(VarNames[2] + "/" + SelectedDataGroup);
			if (iHNode == null || iHNode.Elements.Count == 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			Build_ComponentList(SelectedDataGroup);
			Build_Node_Lookup();
			DetermineMoleTypeViaLine6();
			if (MoleMass == WpfPLRegressionResources.MolePercent_AxisTitle)
			{
				MoleType = WpfPLRegressionResources.MoleFraction_AxisTitle;
			}
			else if (MoleMass == WpfPLRegressionResources.MassPercent_AxisTitle)
			{
				MoleType = WpfPLRegressionResources.MassFraction_AxisTitle;
			}
			else
			{
				MoleType = MoleMass;
			}
			_wizardPlot.PlotVal.Title = string.Format(WpfPLRegressionResources.TriangleS3_Title, SelectedDataGroup, Join(Compons.ToArray(), " ", bTrail: false));
			_wizardPlot.PlotVal.XAxisTitle = (_component1 = ATEXAxisTitle.Text);
			((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_YAxisTitle((object)1, _component2 = ATEYAxisTitle.Text);
			((IHPlotVal)_wizardPlot.PlotVal).set_YAxisTitle((object)0, _component3 = ATEZAxisTitle.Text);
			int num = 0;
			int num2 = 0;
			for (int i = 0; i < 2; i++)
			{
				if (X1c[0, i, 0] != -1.0)
				{
					int num3 = iHNode.Elements.get_RowCount(1);
					if (num3 > num2)
					{
						num2 = num3;
					}
					num++;
				}
				if (X2c[0, i, 0] != -1.0)
				{
					int num4 = iHNode.Elements.get_RowCount(1);
					if (num4 > num2)
					{

		private string SaveConfigValue()
		{
			_ = string.Empty;
			StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
			stringBuilder.Append("VariableSelection=");
			stringBuilder.Append(SelectedDataGroup);
			stringBuilder.Append(";");
			stringBuilder.Append("Comp1=");
			stringBuilder.Append(ATEXAxisTitle.Text.ToString());
			stringBuilder.Append(";");
			stringBuilder.Append("Comp2=");
			stringBuilder.Append(ATEYAxisTitle.Text.ToString());
			stringBuilder.Append(";");
			stringBuilder.Append("Comp3=");
			stringBuilder.Append(ATEZAxisTitle.Text.ToString());
			stringBuilder.Append(";");
			return stringBuilder.ToString();
		}

		private void RestoreConfigValue(string ConfigString)
		{
			string[] array = ConfigString.Split(';');
			int num = 0;
			string selectedDataGroup = array[num].Substring(array[num].IndexOf('=') + 1);
			SelectedDataGroup = selectedDataGroup;
			num++;
			selectedDataGroup = array[num].Substring(array[num].IndexOf('=') + 1);
			ATEXAxisTitle.Text = selectedDataGroup;
			num++;
			selectedDataGroup = array[num].Substring(array[num].IndexOf('=') + 1);
			ATEYAxisTitle.Text = selectedDataGroup;
			num++;
			selectedDataGroup = array[num].Substring(array[num].IndexOf('=') + 1);
			ATEZAxisTitle.Text = selectedDataGroup;
		}

		private void ExecuteOKCommand(object sender, ExecutedRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			UpdateWizardData();
			_help.CloseForm((UserControl)this, (bool?)true);
		}

		private void ExecuteCancelCommand(object sender, ExecutedRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			_help.CloseForm((UserControl)this, (bool?)false);
		}

		private void SetVarNames()
		{
			VarNames = new string[3] { "Output/LINE7", "Output", "Output/Prop Data/PROFILE" };
		}

		private bool Build_CandidateList()
		{
			IHNode iHNode = _context.FindNode("Output/LINE7");
			if (iHNode == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			int count = iHNode.Elements.Count;
			if (count == 0)
			{
				return false;
			}
			ObservableCollection<string> observableCollection = new ObservableCollection<string>();
			for (int i = 0; i < count; i++)
			{
				string item = iHNode.Elements.get_Label(0, i, Type.Missing);
				string text = (dynamic)iHNode.Elements[i, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].Elements["1", Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].get_Value(Type.Missing);
				text = " " + text + " ";
				if (text.Contains(" " + PlotType + " "))
				{
					observableCollection.Add(item);
				}
			}
			DataGroupList = observableCollection;
			if (observableCollection.Count > 0)
			{
				SelectedDataGroup = DataGroupList[0];
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}

		private void Build_ComponentList(string sGroupID)
		{
			Compons.Clear();
			IHNode iHNode = _context.FindNode(VarNames[0] + "/" + sGroupID);
			int count = iHNode.Elements.Count;
			for (int i = 0; i < count; i++)
			{
				string[] array = ((string)(dynamic)iHNode.Elements[i, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].get_Value(Type.Missing)).Split(' ');
				if (array.Length == 0)
				{
					continue;
				}
				List<string> list = new List<string>();
				string[] array2 = array;
				foreach (string text in array2)
				{
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
					{
						list.Add(text);
					}
				}
				if (list.Count > 0 && (list[0] == "X1" || list[0] == "X2"))
				{
					Compons.Add(list[1]);
				}
			}
		}

		private void Build_Node_Lookup()
		{
			int count = Compons.Count;
			Temp = new double[1, 2];
			Pres = new double[1, 2];
			X1c = new double[1, 2, count];
			X2c = new double[1, 2, count];
			for (int i = 0; i < 2; i++)
			{
				Temp[0, i] = -1.0;
				Pres[0, i] = -1.0;
				for (int j = 0; j < count; j++)
				{
					X1c[0, i, j] = -1.0;
					X2c[0, i, j] = -1.0;
				}
			}
			IHNode iHNode = _context.FindNode(VarNames[0] + "/" + SelectedDataGroup);
			int count2 = iHNode.Elements.Count;
			for (int k = 0; k < count2; k++)
			{
				string[] array = ((string)(dynamic)iHNode.Elements[k, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].get_Value(Type.Missing)).Split(' ');
				List<string> list = new List<string>();
				for (int l = 0; l < array.Length; l++)
				{
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(array[l]))
					{
						list.Add(array[l]);
					}
				}
				int num = 0;
				if (list.Count <= 0)
				{
					continue;
				}
				switch (list[0])
				{
				case "T":
					Temp[0, 0] = double.Parse(list[1]) - 1.0;
					if (int.Parse(list[2]) == 2)
					{
						Temp[0, 1] = double.Parse(list[1]);
					}
					break;
				case "P":
					Pres[0, 0] = double.Parse(list[1]) - 1.0;
					if (int.Parse(list[2]) == 2)
					{
						Pres[0, 1] = double.Parse(list[1]);
					}
					break;
				case "X":
					num = Compons.IndexOf(list[1]);
					if (num >= 0)
					{
						X1c[0, 0, num] = double.Parse(list[2]) - 1.0;
						if (int.Parse(list[3]) == 2)
						{
							X1c[0, 1, num] = double.Parse(list[2]);
						}
					}
					break;
				case "X2":
					num = Compons.IndexOf(list[1]);
					if (num >= 0)
					{
						X2c[0, 0, num] = double.Parse(list[2]) - 1.0;
						if (int.Parse(list[3]) == 2)
						{
							X2c[0, 1, num] = double.Parse(list[2]);
						}
					}
					break;
				}
			}
		}

		private string DetermineMoleTypeViaLine6()
		{
			string text = "";
			IHNode iHNode = _context.FindNode("Output/LINE1");
			IHNode iHNode2 = _context.FindNode("Output/LINE6");
			int count = iHNode.Elements.Count;
			for (int i = 0; i < count; i++)
			{
				if (!(SelectedDataGroup == iHNode.Elements[i, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].Elements[0, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].get_Name(Type.Missing)))
				{
					continue;
				}
				string text2 = string.Empty;
				string text3 = string.Empty;
				if (iHNode.Elements[i, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].Elements.Count > 0)
				{
					text2 = (dynamic)iHNode.Elements[i, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].Elements[0, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].get_Value(Type.Missing);
				}
				if (text2 != "TEMP" && text2 != "PRESSURE")
				{
					if (iHNode2.Elements[i, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].Elements.Count > 0)
					{
						text3 = (dynamic)iHNode2.Elements[i, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].Elements[0, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing].get_Value(Type.Missing);
					}
					switch (text3)
					{
					case "MOLEFRAC":
					case "MOLEFRACTION":
						text = WpfPLRegressionResources.MoleFraction_AxisTitle;
						break;
					case "MOLEPCT":
					case "MOLEPERCENT":
						text = WpfPLRegressionResources.MolePercent_AxisTitle;
						break;
					case "MASSFRAC":
					case "MASSFRACTION":
						text = WpfPLRegressionResources.MassFraction_AxisTitle;
						break;
					case "MASSPCT":
					case "MASSPERCENT":
						text = WpfPLRegressionResources.MassPercent_AxisTitle;
						break;
					default:
						text = text2;
						break;
					}
					MoleMass = text;
					return text;
				}
			}
			MoleMass = text;
			return text;
		}

		private bool MT_Present(string mtype)
		{
			string path = "Output/" + mtype;
			return _context.FindNode(path).Elements.Count != 0;
		}

		private void DetermineMoleType()
		{
			if (MT_Present("MOLEFRAC"))
			{
				MoleType = WpfPLRegressionResources.MoleFraction_AxisTitle;
			}
			else if (MT_Present("MOLEPCT"))
			{
				MoleType = WpfPLRegressionResources.MolePercent_AxisTitle;
			}
			else if (MT_Present("MASSFRAC"))
			{
				MoleType = WpfPLRegressionResources.MassFraction_AxisTitle;
			}
			else if (MT_Present("MASSPCT"))
			{
				MoleType = WpfPLRegressionResources.MassPercent_AxisTitle;
			}
			else
			{
				MoleType = "";
			}
		}

		private string Join(string[] xW, string sFill, bool bTrail)
		{
			if (xW == null || xW.Length == 0)
			{
				return string.Empty;
			}
			string text = "";
			for (int i = 0; i < xW.Length; i++)
			{
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(xW[i]))
				{
					text += xW[i];
					if (i != xW.Length - 1 || bTrail)
					{
						text += sFill;
					}
				}
				else if (i == xW.Length - 1 && bTrail && text.Length == 0)
				{
					text = sFill;
				}
			}
			return text;
		}

		public void UpdateComponents(string SelectedDataGroup)
		{
			Build_ComponentList(SelectedDataGroup);
			if (Compons != null && Compons.Count > 0)
			{
				_component1 = MoleType + " " + Compons[0];
				_component2 = Compons[1];
				_component3 = Compons[2];
			}
			ATEXAxisTitle.Text = _component1;
			ATEYAxisTitle.Text = _component2;
			ATEZAxisTitle.Text = _component3;
		}

		{
			if (this.PropertyChanged != null)
			{
				this.PropertyChanged(this, new PropertyChangedEventArgs(propName));
			}
		}
